Borkeo EMR v47.7.0 - ANC to-do list as a clean checklist; the text opens the field to fill
Base 3b255d52bfe6a764735760390e8b8dcd203fadbc (47.6.0)

Antigravity: read docs/development/ANC_CHECKLIST_V477_VALIDATION.md first.
Back up committed code (npm run backup:code), apply these complete UTF-8 payloads, preserve later unrelated customizations.
Never replace .env, hospital databases, uploads, backups or password exports.
Only the ANC record page changes; no data change. Orders moved next to their fields.
Run npm test and npm run check:khmer before restarting only the verified project launcher.
Verified: npm test 1048/1048 passed; npm run check:khmer passed (project folder and disposable copy).

Install, one step at a time:
1. Close the running EMR window (Start-Borkeo-WiFi).
2. This project folder already contains v47.7 (committed and pushed to GitHub master).
   On another computer with a Git copy run: git pull
   Without Git: give this file to Antigravity and ask it to apply the payloads below.
3. Start Start-Borkeo-WiFi.bat again.
4. Open an ANC record and press Ctrl+F5 once so the browser loads the new page.

## Combined approved updates - v47.0.0, 25 October 2026

User explicitly requested every pending discussed update. Base is clean master
cd41efd (46.5.0); development branch feature/unified-anc-workspace. Five pending
minor steps are delivered together as 47.0.0, with package/login/server aligned.
Earlier notes below are historical; registration separation and backups are
now included. Preserve all supplied 46.2-46.5 triage and fixture improvements.

- Registration Save writes registration only and leaves a new patient REGISTERED;
  explicit Send to Triage changes that patient to WAITING. Already transferred
  patients retain their clinical state. Registration chart navigation is hidden.
- OPD plus paraclinic retains the doctor-first ordering flow; OPD alone retains
  direct cashier routing. The notification now explains the correct destination.
- The duplicate OPD consultation editor/navigation and top auxiliary controls
  shown in the screenshot are hidden. Native SOAP remains. Chief complaint is
  editable in Subjective; eight vital measurements and complaint use canonical
  triage fields in the same encounter. Explicit clearing is supported. Existing
  role, ward, payment, discharge and version guards remain; earlier visits and
  protected registration demographics are not overwritten by doctor edits.
- ANC opens one workspace from the old entry point, with current visit,
  pregnancy, tests, care, history, risk and legacy sections. Old-only fields are
  available as supplemental per-visit details. Existing legacy encounter values
  are shown read-only without silently copying a different pregnancy. Record
  and visit Save are atomic; previous encounter links retain their own pregnancy.
- npm run backup:code archives committed code with a checksum. Restore extracts
  into a new folder and never overwrites hospital data or the current app.

The address loader now waits for linked address restoration before enabling
shared editing/saving; stale patient loads cannot apply a prior address/photo.
Independent allergy/problem editors validate their own actions rather than
blocking the main encounter Save with an empty, unrelated draft.

Pre-update verified code snapshot:

## Latest correction - v46.1.0, 25 October 2026

User explicitly authorized fixing Skip for an older patient whose active call
has a valid queue number but registration JSON lacks it. Base: clean fetched
master 838f069. Branch: fix/triage-skip-active-queue-number.

skipQueuePatient retains valid supplied/saved numbers. If they are missing or
invalid it reads the latest active call for exactly the same patient and
normalized service, validates that number, and uses it for the existing skip
operation. It never allocates a new number or rewrites registration/clinical
JSON. A missing valid active number still fails rather than borrowing another
patient, an inactive call or another service. Existing role/ward/CSRF guards,
the skipped-list direct-edit action, admission form and UI are unchanged.

The regression and Chromium fixture now remove both registration queue-number
keys before testing, matching the reported older-record case. The previous
46.0 fixture always had saved numbers and therefore missed this case.
Release versions advance together from 46.0.0 to 46.1.0 / visible 46.1.
See TRIAGE_SKIP_ACTIVE_NUMBER_VALIDATION.md and the combined
Borkeo-EMR-v46.1.0-triage-skip-fix-update.txt. Earlier notes are historical.

# ANC to-do list as a clean checklist v47.7.0

Approved update (owner said "yes" to the v47.7 proposal on 26 October 2026,
keeping the orders next to their fields). Only the ANC record page changes.
Base master 3b255d5 (47.6.0); branch feature/maternity-anc-clean-checklist.

## What changed

- The "to do at this visit" list shows only a tick box and the task text,
  grouped under Records / Laboratory / Examination / Ultrasound / Care
  (ឯកសារ · មន្ទីរពិសោធន៍ · ការពិនិត្យ · អេកូសាស្ត្រ · ការថែទាំ), in 3 / 2 / 1
  aligned columns. No buttons and no cards. Done tasks: green tick, grey text
  (not crossed out); closed tasks: amber text with the reason; overdue: red.
- Clicking the text opens the section of the field where the task is filled,
  scrolls to it, puts the cursor in it and highlights it (TASK_TARGETS in
  public/anc-record.js). Tasks without a field (paper books, additional
  examination, post-term management) tick their box when the text is clicked.
- Tick box: data tasks tick themselves (green, locked). Ticking a data task by
  hand opens the reason window (no test / refused / done elsewhere / other),
  which replaces "close without doing"; Cancel leaves it unticked. Ticking the
  education task also ticks today's "education given" box. Manual tasks: a
  tick means done; unticking a done or closed task reopens it.
- The order actions moved next to their fields: Laboratory order beside
  Hepatitis B, ask a doctor beside CBC and blood group, an OGTT line under the
  laboratory table, Imaging order beside the ultrasound title, add iron to the
  prescription beside the iron field, open the vaccination beside the tetanus
  field. They are not printed.
- The task text is a link (`<a>`), not a button, because the shared button
  style in shared-controls.css (!important) would draw it as a button.

## Backup and data preservation

Pre-update code-only backup (npm run backup:code on the clean baseline):
C:\Users\Local-admin\Borkeo_EMR_Code_Backups\v47.6.0-3b255d52-bfb4f9f6
(commit 3b255d52bfe6a764735760390e8b8dcd203fadbc)

Restore with `npm run restore:code -- "<backup-directory>"` into a NEW folder.
No database or data change; task states are saved as before.

## Verification

- tests/v477-anc-clean-checklist.test.mjs: 5 tests. The v42.6 browser
  regression now closes a task by ticking a data task (reason window).
- Explicit browser check (headless Edge, in-memory database, synthetic
  patient): no button in the list; five headings; clicking FHR, HIV, anaemia
  and the dating ultrasound opened the visit, tests, pregnancy and tests
  sections with the cursor in the right field and the highlight; ticking HIV
  opened the reason window and Cancel left it unticked; the maternal health
  book ticked done; the education task ticked today's box; a data tick could
  not be unticked; all 7 order buttons are next to their fields; no
  JavaScript errors. Screenshot reviewed.
- Full `npm test` and `npm run check:khmer` results are in the update TXT.

consent.procedure.text,"I, the undersigned, the patient or the legal representative of the patient named above, confirm that the doctor has explained to me, in a language I understand: the diagnosis, the proposed procedure, its expected benefits, its possible risks and complications, the other treatment options, and what may happen if the procedure is not done. I had the chance to ask questions and my questions were answered. I agree to the procedure named above, and to any additional procedure that becomes necessary during it to save life or prevent serious harm. // v42.6 - The official ANC record page (/anc-record?patient=...).
// One record per pregnancy (linked to the Health ID), reused at every visit
// until delivery, referral to CPA3 or closing. Pages 2-5 of the Ministry of
// Health record, today's visit (Triage values imported, editable), the colour
// code with its history, the visit schedule and the "to do at this visit"
// panel, the referral to CPA3 and printing (A4 landscape).
import * as anc from '/public/anc-engine.mjs';
import { ANC_REQUIRED_FIELDS, missingAncFields } from '/public/anc-required-fields.mjs';
import { referralLetterHtml } from '/public/anc-referral-letter.mjs';

const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = (value) => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
const english = () => window.getBorkeoLanguage?.() === 'en';
// New UI text: approved Khmer (public/new-text-data.js) or the English placeholder.
const t = (key, en) => (english() ? en : (window.newText ? window.newText(key, en) : en));
const patientKey = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('patient') || '';
let cfg = null;          // GET /api/maternal/config
let data = null;         // GET /api/anc/record
let record = null;       // working copy of the record (pages 2-5)
let draft = {};          // today's visit
let prescription = [];   // today's prescription lines
let tasksShown = false;
let csrf = '';
let activeSection = 'visit';
const sections = () => [
  ['visit', t('anc.todayVisit', "Today's visit")], ['pregnancy', t('anc.sections.pregnancy', 'Pregnancy record')],
  ['tests', t('anc.sections.tests', 'Tests and ultrasound')], ['care', t('anc.sections.care', 'Care and treatment')],
  ['history', t('anc.sections.history', 'Visit history')], ['risk', t('anc.sections.risk', 'Risk assessment')],
  ['legacy', t('anc.sections.legacy', 'Previous ANC form')],
];
function selectSection(name, focus = false) {
  const available = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-anc-section]')].map(node => node.dataset.ancSection);
  activeSection = available.includes(name) ? name : (available[0] || 'legacy');
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-anc-section]').forEach(node => { node.hidden = node.dataset.ancSection !== activeSection; });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-anc-nav]').forEach(node => {
    const selected = node.dataset.ancNav === activeSection;
    node.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(selected)); node.tabIndex = selected ? 0 : -1;
    if (selected && focus) node.focus();
  });
}
function renderNavigation() {
  const host = $('anc-sections');
  host.setAttribute('aria-label', L().title);
  host.setAttribute('role', 'tablist');
  host.innerHTML = sections().filter(([name]) => document.querySelector(`[data-anc-section="${name}"]`)).map(([name, label]) =>
    `<button type="button" role="tab" id="anc-nav-${name}" data-anc-nav="${name}" aria-controls="anc-pane-${name}">${esc(label)}</button>`).join('');
  selectSection(activeSection);
}
const pane = (name, content) => `<div id="anc-pane-${name}" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="anc-nav-${name}" data-anc-section="${name}">${content}</div>`;
function legacySection() {
  const legacy = data.legacyAnc;
  return `<section class="anc-page anc-legacy"><h2>${esc(t('anc.sections.legacy', 'Previous ANC form'))}</h2>
    <p class="anc-hint">${esc(t('anc.legacyNotice', 'Saved values from this encounter are read only and are not copied into another pregnancy.'))}</p>
    <p>${esc(legacy?.caseNumber || '')} · ${esc(legacy?.date || '')}</p>
    ${legacy?.entries?.length ? `<dl class="anc-legacy-values">${legacy.entries.map(item => `<div><dt>${esc(item.label)}</dt><dd>${esc(item.value)}</dd></div>`).join('')}</dl>`
      : `<p>${esc(t('anc.legacyEmpty', 'No saved old ANC form for this encounter.'))}</p>`}</section>`;
}
function supplementalVisit() {
  const starts = [32, 34, 37, 41, 43, 44, 45, 47, 49, 51, 52, 53, 54];
  const fields = cfg.legacyFields || [];
  return `<section class="anc-page anc-supplemental"><h2>${esc(t('anc.extraVisit', 'Additional visit details'))}</h2><div class="anc-visit-grid anc-grid">${starts.map(index => {
    const field = fields.find(item => item.key === `scope:medical:auto:${index}`);
    if (!field) return '';
    const value = draft.legacyDetails?.[index] || '';
    const options = [37, 44].includes(index) ? null : index === 41 ? Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => String(i + 1)) : field.type === 'radio' ? fields.filter(item => item.name === field.name).map(item => item.option) : field.options;
    const control = options ? `<select data-visit="legacyDetails.${index}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}><option value=""></option>${options.map(option => `<option value="${esc(option)}"${value === option ? ' selected' : ''}>${esc(option)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`
      : vfield(`legacyDetails.${index}`, { type: field.type === 'date' ? 'date' : field.type === 'number' ? 'number' : 'text' });
    const vaccine = index === 44 ? `<a class="link anc-order no-print" href="/vaccination?patient=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}&vaccine=tt">${esc(t('anc.giveVaccine', 'Open the vaccination'))}</a>` : '';
    return cell(esc(field.label), control, { after: vaccine });
  }).join('')}</div></section>`;
}

async function api(path, body) {
  if (body && !csrf) csrf = (await fetch('/api/auth/me', { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' }).then((r) => r.json())).csrfToken;
  const response = await fetch(path, body ? { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': csrf }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }
    : { credentials: 'same-origin', cache: 'no-store' });
  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) {
    const fallback = response.status === 409 ? t('anc.reloadConflict', 'The record changed or is closed. Reload it.')
      : response.status === 403 ? t('anc.accessDenied', 'You do not have permission for this action.') : t('anc.requestFailed', 'The request failed. Please try again.');
    throw Object.assign(new Error(english() ? result.error || fallback : fallback), { body: result, status: response.status });
  }
  return result;
}

const L = () => cfg.labels;
const setStatus = (text, error = false) => { const node = $('anc-status'); node.textContent = text || ''; node.dataset.state = error ? 'error' : 'ok'; };
const canEdit = () => Boolean(data?.canEdit && data?.pregnancy?.status === 'open');
const colourWord = (colour) => L().colours[colour] || colour;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths
function getPath(object, path) {
  return path.split('.').reduce((value, key) => (value == null ? undefined : value[key]), object);
}
function setPath(object, path, value) {
  const keys = path.split('.');
  let node = object;
  keys.slice(0, -1).forEach((key, index) => {
    if (node[key] == null || typeof node[key] !== 'object') node[key] = key !== 'legacyDetails' && /^\d+$/.test(keys[index + 1]) ? [] : {};
    node = node[key];
  });
  node[keys[keys.length - 1]] = value;
}
const field = (path, { type = 'text', size = '', placeholder = '' } = {}) => {
  const value = getPath(record, path);
  if (type === 'checkbox') return `<input type="checkbox" data-path="${esc(path)}"${value ? ' checked' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>`;
  return `<input type="${type}" data-path="${esc(path)}" value="${esc(value ?? '')}" class="anc-field ${size}" placeholder="${esc(placeholder)}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>`;
};
const choice = (path, value, label) => `<label class="anc-choice"><input type="radio" name="${esc(path)}" data-path="${esc(path)}" value="${esc(value)}"${getPath(record, path) === value ? ' checked' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}> ${esc(label)}</label>`;
const vfield = (key, { type = 'text', size = '' } = {}) => `<input type="${type}" data-visit="${esc(key)}" value="${esc(getPath(draft, key) ?? '')}" class="anc-field ${size}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>`;
// v47.3 - Dropdown lists (stored as codes). A value typed before v47.3 that is
// not in the list stays as its own option, so nothing saved is lost.
const PRESENTATION_TEXT = { cephalic: ['anc.pres.cephalic', 'Cephalic'], breech: ['anc.pres.breech', 'Breech'], transverse: ['anc.pres.transverse', 'Transverse'] };
const PLACENTA_TEXT = { anterior: ['anc.placenta.anterior', 'Anterior'], posterior: ['anc.placenta.posterior', 'Posterior'], fundal: ['anc.placenta.fundal', 'Fundal'],
  lateral: ['anc.placenta.lateral', 'Lateral'], previa: ['anc.placenta.previa', 'Placenta previa'] };
const FLUID_TEXT = { normal: ['anc.fluid.normal', 'Normal'], low: ['anc.fluid.low', 'Low'], high: ['anc.fluid.high', 'High'] };
const codeText = (table, value) => (table[value] ? t(...table[value]) : value);
const OPTION_LISTS = {
  presentation: () => anc.PRESENTATIONS.map((code) => [code, codeText(PRESENTATION_TEXT, code)]),
  placenta: () => anc.PLACENTAS.map((code) => [code, codeText(PLACENTA_TEXT, code)]),
  afi: () => anc.FLUIDS.map((code) => [code, codeText(FLUID_TEXT, code)]),
  fetuses: () => anc.FETUSES.map((code) => [code, code]),
  proteinuria: () => anc.PROTEINURIA.map((code) => [code, code]),
  glucosuria: () => anc.GLUCOSURIA.map((code) => [code, code]),
};
const pickList = (attr, path, value, list) => {
  const current = String(value ?? '');
  const options = [['', ''], ...list, ...(current && !list.some(([code]) => code === current) ? [[current, current]] : [])];
  return `<select ${attr}="${esc(path)}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>${options.map(([code, text]) => `<option value="${esc(code)}"${code === current ? ' selected' : ''}>${esc(text)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
};
const recordPick = (path, list) => pickList('data-path', path, getPath(record, path), list);
const visitPick = (key, list) => pickList('data-visit', key, getPath(draft, key), list);
// v47.6 - One field = one cell: label on top, the box and its unit below (screen
// grid in anc-record.css). Spans are inline, so the printed record keeps its flow.
const cell = (labelHtml, control, { unit = '', span = 1, check = false, after = '' } = {}) =>
  `<label class="anc-cell${span === 2 ? ' anc-span-2' : ''}${check ? ' anc-cell-check' : ''}"><span class="anc-cell-label">${labelHtml}</span> <span class="anc-cell-input">${control}${unit ? ` <span class="anc-unit">${esc(unit)}</span>` : ''}${after ? ` ${after}` : ''}</span></label>`;
const perMinute = () => t('anc.unit.perMinute', '/min');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- status
function visitsWithDraft() {
  const saved = (data?.visits || []).filter((visit) => !(visit.date === data.today && visit.patientKey === patientKey));
  return [...saved, { ...draft, date: data.today, patientKey }].sort((a, b) => String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));
}
function currentStatus() {
  const labs = [...(data.released?.labs || []), ...(record.labs || [])];
  const ultrasounds = [...(data.released?.ultrasounds || []), ...(record.ultrasounds || [])];
  return anc.ancStatus({ chart: cfg.chart, schedule: cfg.schedule, record: { ...record, labs, ultrasounds }, visits: visitsWithDraft(), today: data.today, options: cfg.options, vaccinations: data.vaccinations || [] });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- top and summary
function renderTop(status) {
  $('anc-title').textContent = L().title;
  document.title = L().title;
  const colour = status?.colour || '';
  $('anc-colour-boxes').innerHTML = ['green', 'yellow', 'red'].map((key) => `<span class="anc-colour-box anc-box-${key}${colour === key ? ' is-ticked' : ''}" data-colour="${key}"><span class="anc-box" aria-hidden="true">${colour === key ? '✓' : ''}</span>${esc(colourWord(key))}</span>`).join('');
}

function renderSummary(status) {
  const host = $('anc-summary');
  if (!data.pregnancy) { host.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  const ga = status.ga ? `${status.ga.label} ${t('anc.weeks', 'weeks')}` : '—';
  const source = status.dating.source === 'ultrasound' ? t('anc.sourceUltrasound', 'from the dating ultrasound') : status.dating.source === 'lmp' ? t('anc.sourceLmp', 'from the last menstrual period') : '';
  const colourNote = status.colour === 'yellow' ? t('anc.yellowNote', 'ANC continues at Borkeo Referral Hospital (CPA2) - 8 visits')
    : status.colour === 'red' ? t('anc.redNote', 'Refer to the provincial hospital (CPA3) with a referral form signed by a doctor') : t('anc.greenNote', 'ANC with the midwife - 4 visits');
  const pendingReferral = (data.visits || []).find((visit) => visit.referral?.requestedAt && !visit.referral?.signed);
  const signedReferral = [...(data.visits || [])].reverse().find((visit) => visit.referral?.signed);
  const closed = data.pregnancy.status !== 'open' ? `<span class="anc-closed">${esc(t(`anc.status.${data.pregnancy.status}`, data.pregnancy.status))}</span>` : '';
  host.innerHTML = `
    <div class="anc-summary-line"><strong>${esc(data.patient.fullName)}</strong> · ${esc(data.patient.healthId || data.patient.caseNumber || '')} ${closed}</div>
    <div class="anc-summary-line">${esc(L().identity.edd)} <strong>${esc(status.dating.edd || '—')}</strong> <small>${esc(source)}</small> · ${esc(t('anc.ga', 'Gestational age'))} <strong>${esc(ga)}</strong></div>
    <div class="anc-colour-note anc-note-${esc(status.colour)}">${esc(colourNote)}${status.flags.includes('height-check') ? ` · ${esc(t('anc.heightCheck', 'Height under 145 cm: check'))}` : ''}</div>
    ${status.reasons.length ? `<ul class="anc-reasons">${status.reasons.map((reason) => `<li class="anc-reason-${esc(reason.level)}">${esc(reason.kh)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>` : ''}
    <div class="anc-actions no-print">
      ${data.canEdit && data.pregnancy.status !== 'open' && data.pregnancy.patientKey !== patientKey && !(data.visits || []).some(visit => visit.patientKey === patientKey) ? `<button type="button" data-action="open">${esc(t('anc.open', 'Open the ANC record'))}</button>` : ''}
      ${canEdit() ? `<button type="button" data-action="save-visit">${esc(t('anc.saveVisit', 'Save the visit'))}</button>` : ''}
      <button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="print">${esc(t('form.print', 'Print'))}</button>
      ${canEdit() && status.colour === 'red' && !pendingReferral ? `<button type="button" class="danger" data-action="referral">${esc(t('anc.requestReferral', 'Refer to CPA3 (doctor signs)'))}</button>` : ''}
      ${pendingReferral && data.canSignReferral ? `<button type="button" class="danger" data-action="sign">${esc(t('anc.signReferral', 'Sign the referral'))}</button>` : ''}
      ${pendingReferral && !data.canSignReferral ? `<span class="anc-pending">${esc(t('anc.referralPending', 'Referral waiting for a doctor signature'))}</span>` : ''}
      ${signedReferral ? `<button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="print-referral">${esc(t('anc.printReferral', 'Print the referral letter'))}</button>` : ''}
      ${canEdit() ? `<button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="close">${esc(t('anc.closeRecord', 'Close the record'))}</button>` : ''}
    </div>`;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- page 2
function page2() {
  const l = L();
  const id = l.identity;
  const yesNo = (path) => `${choice(path, 'no', l.gyn.none)} ${choice(path, 'yes', l.gyn.yes)} ${field(`${path}Text`, { size: 'wide' })}`;
  const bmiValue = anc.bmi(record.prePregnancyWeightKg, record.heightCm);
  return `<section class="anc-page" data-page="2">
    <h2>${esc(l.title)}</h2>
    <div class="anc-box-panel anc-identity">
      ${cell(esc(id.name), field('identity.name', { size: 'wide' }), { span: 2 })}
      ${cell(esc(id.dateOfBirth), field('identity.dateOfBirth', { type: 'date' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.age), field('identity.age', { size: 'tiny' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.gravida), field('identity.gravida', { size: 'tiny' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.births), field('identity.births', { size: 'tiny' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.miscarriages), field('identity.miscarriages', { size: 'tiny' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.abortions), field('identity.abortions', { size: 'tiny' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.lmp), field('lmp', { type: 'date' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.edd), `<output>${esc(anc.eddFromLmp(record.lmp) || '—')}</output>`)}
      ${cell(esc(id.eddUltrasound), field('ultrasoundEdd', { type: 'date' }))}
      ${cell(`<small>${esc(t('anc.scanDate', 'scan date'))}</small>`, field('ultrasoundDate', { type: 'date' }))}
      ${cell(esc(id.height), field('heightCm', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: id.heightUnit })}
      ${cell(esc(id.weightBefore), field('prePregnancyWeightKg', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: 'kg', after: `<small>${esc(record.prePregnancyWeightSource === 'triage' ? t('anc.fromTriage', 'from Triage') : '')}</small>` })}
      ${cell(esc(id.bmiBefore), `<output>${esc(bmiValue ?? '—')}</output>`, { span: 2, after: `<small>${esc(id.bmiNormal)}</small>` })}
    </div>
    <div class="anc-two">
      <div class="anc-box-panel">
        <p class="anc-check-block"><strong>• ${esc(l.general.title)}</strong> <span class="anc-check-grid">${Object.entries(l.general.items).map(([key, label]) => `<label class="anc-choice">${field(`history.general.${key}`, { type: 'checkbox' })} ${esc(label)}</label>`).join(' ')}</span> ${field('history.general.other', { size: 'wide' })}</p>
        <p><strong>• ${esc(l.surgery.title)}</strong></p>
        ${l.surgery.numbers.map((number, index) => `<p class="anc-indent anc-entry anc-entry-surgery">${esc(number)} ${esc(l.surgery.diagnosis)} ${field(`history.surgery.${index}.diagnosis`, { size: 'wide' })} ${esc(l.surgery.year)} ${field(`history.surgery.${index}.year`, { size: 'tiny' })} ${esc(l.surgery.place)} ${field(`history.surgery.${index}.place`)}</p>`).join('')}
        <p><strong>• ${esc(l.allergy.title)}</strong></p>
        ${l.allergy.numbers.map((number, index) => `<p class="anc-indent anc-entry anc-entry-allergy">${esc(number)} ${esc(l.allergy.drug)} ${field(`history.allergy.${index}.drug`)} ${esc(l.allergy.symptom)} ${field(`history.allergy.${index}.symptom`, { size: 'wide' })}</p>`).join('')}
        <p class="anc-hint">${esc(t('anc.allergyLink', 'Drug allergies are also kept in the structured allergy record (patient banner).'))} ${(data.allergies || []).map((a) => esc(a)).join(', ')}</p>
        <p class="anc-check-block"><strong>• ${esc(l.family.title)}</strong> <span class="anc-check-grid">${Object.entries(l.family.items).map(([key, label]) => `<label class="anc-choice">${field(`history.family.${key}`, { type: 'checkbox' })} ${esc(label)}</label>`).join(' ')}</span> ${field('history.family.otherText', { size: 'wide' })}</p>
        <p class="anc-form-row"><strong>• ${esc(l.gyn.title)}</strong> <span class="anc-row-label">${esc(l.gyn.menses)}</span> <span class="anc-row-controls">${choice('history.gyn.menses', 'regular', l.gyn.regular)} ${choice('history.gyn.menses', 'irregular', l.gyn.irregular)}
          <span class="anc-group">${esc(l.gyn.cycle)} ${field('history.gyn.cycleDays', { size: 'tiny' })} ${esc(l.gyn.day)}</span> <span class="anc-group">${esc(l.gyn.duration)} ${field('history.gyn.durationDays', { size: 'tiny' })}${esc(l.gyn.perMonth)}</span></span></p>
        <p class="anc-indent anc-form-row"><span class="anc-row-label">${esc(l.gyn.contraception)}</span> <span class="anc-row-controls">${choice('history.gyn.contraception', 'no', l.gyn.none)} <span class="anc-group">(${esc(l.gyn.reason)} ${field('history.gyn.contraceptionReason')})</span> ${choice('history.gyn.contraception', 'yes', l.gyn.yes)} ${field('history.gyn.contraceptionText')}</span></p>
        <p class="anc-indent anc-form-row"><span class="anc-row-label">${esc(l.gyn.art)}</span> <span class="anc-row-controls">${choice('history.gyn.art', 'no', l.gyn.none)} ${choice('history.gyn.art', 'yes', l.gyn.yes)}</span> <span class="anc-row-label">${esc(l.gyn.otherGyn)}</span> <span class="anc-row-controls">${field('history.gyn.otherGyn', { size: 'wide' })}</span></p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div class="anc-box-panel">
          <p class="anc-check-block"><strong>• ${esc(l.social.title)}</strong> <span class="anc-check-grid">${['smoking', 'alcohol', 'drugs'].map((key) => `<label class="anc-choice">${field(`history.social.${key}`, { type: 'checkbox' })} ${esc(l.social[key])}</label>`).join(' ')}</span> ${field('history.social.other')}</p>
          <p class="anc-yesno-row anc-yesno-title"><strong>• ${esc(l.violence.title)}</strong> ${yesNo('history.violence')}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="anc-box-panel">
          <p><strong>• ${esc(l.mouth.title)}</strong></p>
          ${['stomatitis', 'caries', 'breast'].map((key) => `<p class="anc-indent anc-yesno-row"><span class="anc-yesno-q">${esc(l.mouth[key])}</span> ${yesNo(`history.mouth.${key}`)}</p>`).join('')}
          <p><strong>• ${esc(l.anaemia.title)}</strong></p>
          ${['eyelid', 'nails', 'palms'].map((key) => `<p class="anc-indent anc-yesno-row"><span class="anc-yesno-q">${esc(l.anaemia[key])}</span> ${yesNo(`history.anaemia.${key}`)}</p>`).join('')}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    ${previousTable()}
  </section>`;
}

function previousTable() {
  const p = L().previous;
  const rows = Array.from({ length: Math.max(5, (record.previous || []).length + 1) }, (_, index) => index);
  const cols = ['num', 'date', 'tick', 'tick', ...Array(9).fill('std')];
  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(p.title)}</h3><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table anc-fixed-table anc-prev-table">
    <colgroup>${cols.map((kind) => `<col class="anc-col-${kind}">`).join('')}</colgroup>
    <thead><tr><th rowspan="2">${esc(p.number)}</th><th rowspan="2">${esc(p.date)}</th><th colspan="2">${esc(p.abortionGroup)}</th><th colspan="5">${esc(p.motherGroup)}</th><th colspan="4">${esc(p.babyGroup)}</th></tr>
    <tr><th>${esc(p.under12)}</th><th>${esc(p.over12)}</th><th>${esc(p.term)}</th><th>${esc(p.deliveryType)}</th><th>${esc(p.place)}</th><th>${esc(p.complications)}</th><th>${esc(p.healthProblems)}</th>
    <th>${esc(p.babyOutcome)}</th><th>${esc(p.sex)}</th><th>${esc(p.weight)}</th><th>${esc(p.babyProblems)}</th></tr></thead>
    <tbody>${rows.map((index) => `<tr><td>${index + 1}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.date`, { type: 'date' })}</td>
      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.under12`, { type: 'checkbox' })}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.over12`, { type: 'checkbox' })}</td>
      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.term`, { size: 'small' })}</td>
      <td><select data-path="previous.${index}.deliveryType"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>${['', 'normal', 'caesarean', 'vacuum'].map((value) => `<option value="${value}"${getPath(record, `previous.${index}.deliveryType`) === value ? ' selected' : ''}>${esc(value ? t(`anc.delivery.${value}`, value) : '')}</option>`).join('')}</select></td>
      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.place`, { size: 'small' })}</td>
      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.complications`, { size: 'small', placeholder: '1-10' })}</td>
      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.healthProblems`, { size: 'small' })}</td>
      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.babyOutcome`, { size: 'small' })}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.sex`, { size: 'tiny' })}</td>
      <td>${field(`previous.${index}.weight`, { size: 'tiny' })}</td><td>${field(`previous.${index}.babyProblems`, { size: 'small' })}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
    <p class="anc-footnote">*${p.complicationList.map((item, index) => `${index + 1}.${esc(item)}`).join(' ')}</p></div>`;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- page 3
const LAB_ORDERS = { hbsag: 'HBSAG', hb: 'CBC', group: 'BLOOD_GROUP' };
function labTable() {
  const l = L().labs;
  const released = data.released?.labs || [];
  const rapid = (data.visits || []).filter((visit) => visit.rapid && Object.values(visit.rapid).some(Boolean)).map((visit) => ({ date: visit.date, source: 'rapid', ...visit.rapid }));
  const typed = Array.from({ length: Math.max(4, (record.labs || []).length) }, (_, index) => index);
  const rows = [['hiv', l.hiv], ['syphilis', l.syphilis], ['hbsag', l.hbsag], ['hcv', l.hcv], ['hb', `${l.redCells} ${l.hb}`], ['hct', l.hct], ['plt', l.plt], ['group', l.group]];
  const freeLabels = record.labFreeLabels || [];
  const readCell = (column, key) => `<td class="anc-readonly anc-src-${esc(column.source)}">${esc(column[key] || '')}</td>`;
  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(l.title)}</h3><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table anc-fixed-table anc-lab-table">
    <tbody>
      <tr><th>${esc(l.date)}</th>${released.map((c) => readCell(c, 'date')).join('')}${rapid.map((c) => readCell(c, 'date')).join('')}${typed.map((i) => `<td>${field(`labs.${i}.date`, { type: 'date' })}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
      ${rows.map(([key, label]) => `<tr><th>${esc(label)}${LAB_ORDERS[key] ? ` ${orderButton(LAB_ORDERS[key])}` : ''}</th>${released.map((c) => readCell(c, key)).join('')}${rapid.map((c) => readCell(c, key)).join('')}${typed.map((i) => `<td>${field(`labs.${i}.${key}`, { size: 'small' })}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
      ${[0, 1, 2].map((n) => `<tr><th>${field(`labFreeLabels.${n}`, { placeholder: '…' })}</th>${released.map(() => '<td></td>').join('')}${rapid.map(() => '<td></td>').join('')}${typed.map((i) => `<td>${field(`labs.${i}.free${n}`, { size: 'small' })}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
    </tbody></table></div>
    ${canEdit() ? `<p class="anc-order-line no-print">OGTT ${orderButton('OGTT')}</p>` : ''}<p class="anc-hint">${esc(t('anc.labsHint', 'Released Laboratory results and the midwife\'s rapid tests are filled in automatically.'))}</p></div>`;
}

function ultrasoundTable() {
  const u = L().ultrasound;
  const released = data.released?.ultrasounds || [];
  const typed = Array.from({ length: Math.max(3, (record.ultrasounds || []).length + 1) }, (_, index) => index);
  const units = { ga: t('anc.weeks', 'weeks'), bpd: 'mm', weight: t('anc.unit.grams', 'g') };
  const cell = (i, key) => (OPTION_LISTS[key] ? recordPick(`ultrasounds.${i}.${key}`, OPTION_LISTS[key]())
    : field(`ultrasounds.${i}.${key}`, { type: ['date', 'edd'].includes(key) ? 'date' : 'text', size: 'small' }));
  const shown = (key, value) => (key === 'presentation' ? codeText(PRESENTATION_TEXT, value) : key === 'placenta' ? codeText(PLACENTA_TEXT, value) : key === 'afi' ? codeText(FLUID_TEXT, value) : value);
  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(u.title)} ${orderButton('ULTRASOUND')}</h3><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table anc-fixed-table anc-us-table">
    <colgroup>${u.columns.map((c) => `<col class="anc-col-${['date', 'edd'].includes(c.key) ? 'date' : 'std'}">`).join('')}</colgroup>
    <thead><tr>${u.columns.map((c) => `<th>${esc(english() ? c.en : c.kh)}${units[c.key] ? `<br><small>(${esc(units[c.key])})</small>` : ''}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
    <tbody>${released.map((row) => `<tr class="anc-readonly">${u.columns.map((c) => `<td>${esc(shown(c.key, row[c.key] || ''))}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
    ${typed.map((i) => `<tr>${u.columns.map((c) => `<td>${cell(i, c.key)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
}

function visitValue(visit, key, status) {
  if (key === 'date') return visit.date;
  if (key === 'ga') return visit.gaWeeks != null ? `${Math.floor(visit.gaWeeks)}+${Math.round((visit.gaWeeks % 1) * 7)}` : '';
  if (key === 'bp') return [visit.bp, visit.pulse].filter(Boolean).join(' - ');
  if (key === 'name') return visit.staffName || visit.createdBy || '';
  if (key === 'vaccination') return (data.vaccinations || []).filter((v) => v.givenDate === visit.date).map((v) => `TT${v.doseNumber}`).join(', ');
  if (key === 'wp') return visit.wp || weightGain(visit.weight);
  if (key === 'fp') return codeText(PRESENTATION_TEXT, visit.fp ?? '');
  return visit[key] ?? '';
}
function weightGain(weight) {
  const before = Number(record.prePregnancyWeightKg), now = Number(weight);
  return before > 0 && now > 0 ? String(Math.round((now - before) * 10) / 10) : '';
}

function visitTable() {
  const columns = L().visits.columns;
  return `<div class="anc-box-panel"><div class="anc-table-wrap"><table class="anc-table anc-visit-table">
    <thead><tr>${columns.map((c) => `<th>${esc(english() ? c.en : c.kh)}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
    <tbody>${(data.visits || []).map((visit) => `<tr class="${visit.referred ? 'anc-referred' : ''}">${columns.map((c) => `<td>${esc(visitValue(visit, c.key))}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')
      || `<tr><td colspan="${columns.length}">—</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- today's visit
function visitForm(status) {
  const cols = Object.fromEntries(L().visits.columns.map((c) => [c.key, c]));
  const label = (key) => esc(english() ? cols[key].en : cols[key].kh);
  const imported = data.imported || {};
  const visitName = status.scheduled ? `${t('anc.visitOf', 'Visit')} ${status.visit.visit}/${status.visitsPlanned} · ${status.visit.label}` : t('anc.notScheduled', 'Not a scheduled visit');
  const ironOptions = (cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).map((item) => `<option value="${esc(item.id)}">${esc(item.label)}</option>`).join('');
  return `<section class="anc-page anc-visit" id="anc-visit-form">
    <h2>${esc(t('anc.todayVisit', "Today's visit"))} · ${esc(data.today)} · <span class="anc-visit-name">${esc(visitName)}</span></h2>
    <p>${esc(cfg.legacyFields?.find(item => item.key === 'scope:medical:auto:39')?.label || t('anc.ga', 'GA'))}<span class="anc-required-mark" aria-hidden="true"> *</span> <output data-anc-ga>${esc(status.ga ? status.ga.label : '—')}</output></p>
    <p class="anc-hint">${esc(t('anc.importedHint', 'Values from Registration and from Triage of this visit; the midwife can correct them (the Triage value stays saved).'))}
      ${esc([imported.weightKg && `${imported.weightKg} kg`, imported.bp && `${imported.bp} mmHg`, imported.pulse && `${imported.pulse}/min`, imported.temperature && `${imported.temperature} °C`, imported.spo2 && `SpO2 ${imported.spo2}%`].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p>
    <div class="anc-visit-grid anc-grid">
      ${cell(label('weight'), vfield('weight', { size: 'small' }), { unit: 'kg' })}
      ${cell(label('wp'), `<output>${esc(weightGain(draft.weight) || '—')}</output>`, { unit: 'kg' })}
      ${cell(esc(t('anc.bp', 'Blood pressure')), vfield('bp', { size: 'small' }), { unit: 'mmHg' })}
      ${cell(esc(t('anc.pulse', 'Pulse')), vfield('pulse', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: perMinute() })}
      ${cell(esc(t('anc.temperature', 'Temperature')), vfield('temperature', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: '°C' })}
      ${cell('SpO2', vfield('spo2', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: '%' })}
      ${cell(label('uh'), vfield('uh', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: english() ? 'cm' : L().identity.heightUnit })}
      ${cell(label('fp'), visitPick('fp', OPTION_LISTS.presentation()))}
      ${cell(label('fhr'), vfield('fhr', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: perMinute() })}
      ${cell(label('proteinuria'), visitPick('proteinuria', OPTION_LISTS.proteinuria()))}
      ${cell(label('glucosuria'), visitPick('glucosuria', OPTION_LISTS.glucosuria()))}
      ${cell(label('varices'), vfield('varices', { size: 'small' }))}
      ${cell(label('fer'), vfield('fer', { size: 'tiny' }), { after: canEdit() ? `<button type="button" class="link anc-order no-print" data-action="rx-iron">${esc(t('anc.addIron', 'Add to the prescription'))}</button>` : '' })}
      ${cell(esc(t('anc.educationDone', 'Education given')), `<input type="checkbox" data-visit="educationDone"${draft.educationDone === true ? ' checked' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>`, { check: true })}
    </div>
    <fieldset class="anc-rapid"><legend>${esc(t('anc.rapidTests', 'Rapid tests done by the midwife'))}</legend>
      <div class="anc-grid">${cell('HIV', rapidSelect('hiv'))}${cell('Syphilis', rapidSelect('syphilis'))}${cell('Hb', vfield('rapid.hb', { size: 'tiny' }), { unit: 'g/dL' })}</div>
    </fieldset>
    <div class="anc-visit-grid anc-grid">
      ${cell(label('complaint'), vfield('complaint', { size: 'wide' }), { span: 2 })}
      ${cell(label('diagnosis'), vfield('diagnosis', { size: 'wide' }), { span: 2 })}
      ${cell(label('appointment'), vfield('appointment', { type: 'date' }), { span: 2, unit: `${t('anc.suggested', 'suggested')}: ${status.appointment || '—'}` })}
      ${cell(label('name'), `<output>${esc(window.emrSession?.user?.displayName || '')}</output>`, { span: 2 })}
    </div>
    <fieldset class="anc-rx"><legend>${esc(t('anc.prescription', 'Prescription (maternal services medicines)'))}</legend>
      <table class="anc-table"><tbody>${prescription.map((line, index) => `<tr><td>${esc(line.name)}</td><td><input class="anc-field tiny" data-rx="${index}" data-rx-key="doseAmount" value="${esc(line.doseAmount)}"> ${esc(line.unit)}</td>
        <td><select data-rx="${index}" data-rx-key="scheduleType">${['qd_m', 'bid', 'tid'].map((value) => `<option value="${value}"${line.scheduleType === value ? ' selected' : ''}>${value.toUpperCase().replace('_M', '')}</option>`).join('')}</select></td>
        <td><input class="anc-field tiny" data-rx="${index}" data-rx-key="d" value="${esc(line.d)}"> ${esc(t('anc.days', 'days'))}</td><td>${esc(rxTotal(line))}</td>
        <td><button type="button" class="link" data-rx-remove="${index}">✕</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>
      ${canEdit() ? `<select id="anc-rx-pick"><option value=""></option>${ironOptions}</select> <button type="button" class="secondary" data-action="rx-add">+</button>
        <button type="button" data-action="rx-send"${prescription.length ? '' : ' disabled'}>${esc(t('anc.sendPharmacy', 'Send to Pharmacy'))}</button>` : ''}
    </fieldset>
    ${canEdit() ? `<div class="anc-actions">
      <a class="secondary-link" href="/vaccination?patient=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}">${esc(t('anc.giveVaccine', 'Open the vaccination'))}</a></div>` : ''}
  </section>`;
}
function rapidSelect(key) {
  const value = getPath(draft, `rapid.${key}`) || '';
  return `<select data-visit="rapid.${key}"${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>${[['', ''], ['Negative', t('anc.negative', 'Negative')], ['Positive', t('anc.positive', 'Positive')]].map(([v, text]) => `<option value="${v}"${value === v ? ' selected' : ''}>${esc(text)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
}
const PER_DAY = { qd_m: 1, bid: 2, tid: 3 };
const rxTotal = (line) => { const total = Number(line.doseAmount) * (PER_DAY[line.scheduleType] || 1) * Number(line.d); return total > 0 ? String(total) : ''; };

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- colour chart and history
// v47.3: a box is ticked when its item applies - from the patient data
// (Registration, Triage, this record, results; locked, the data is corrected
// instead) or ticked by the midwife.
function chartSection(status) {
  const applied = new Set(status.applied);
  const automatic = new Set(status.automatic);
  return `<section class="anc-page anc-chart"><h2>${esc(cfg.chart.title)}</h2>
    <div class="anc-chart-grid">${cfg.chart.sections.map((section) => `<div class="anc-box-panel"><h3>${esc(section.kh)}</h3>
      ${['green', 'yellow', 'red'].map((level) => `<div class="anc-chart-level anc-level-${level}"><strong>${esc(cfg.chart.levels[level])}</strong>
        ${section.items.filter((item) => item.level === level).map((item) => `<label class="anc-choice${applied.has(item.key) ? ' is-applied' : ''}${automatic.has(item.key) ? ' is-automatic' : ''}">
          <input type="checkbox" data-path="ticks.${esc(item.key)}"${applied.has(item.key) ? ' checked' : ''}${automatic.has(item.key) ? ' data-auto="1" aria-readonly="true"' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}> ${esc(item.kh)}</label>`).join('')}</div>`).join('')}
    </div>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
}
function refreshChart(status) {
  const applied = new Set(status.applied);
  const automatic = new Set(status.automatic);
  document.querySelectorAll('.anc-chart input[data-path^="ticks."]').forEach((input) => {
    const key = input.dataset.path.slice(6);
    input.checked = applied.has(key);
    input.disabled = !canEdit();
    if (automatic.has(key)) { input.dataset.auto = '1'; input.setAttribute('aria-readonly', 'true'); } else { delete input.dataset.auto; input.removeAttribute('aria-readonly'); }
    input.closest('.anc-choice')?.classList.toggle('is-applied', applied.has(key));
    input.closest('.anc-choice')?.classList.toggle('is-automatic', automatic.has(key));
  });
}

function historySection() {
  const history = data.colourHistory || [];
  return `<section class="anc-page anc-history"><h2>${esc(t('anc.colourHistory', 'Colour changes'))}</h2>
    <ul>${history.map((entry) => `<li><span class="anc-dot anc-${esc(entry.colour)}"></span> ${esc(new Date(entry.changedAt).toLocaleString())} · <strong>${esc(colourWord(entry.colour))}</strong> · ${esc(entry.changedBy)}
      ${entry.reasons.length ? `<br><small>${entry.reasons.map((reason) => esc(reason.kh)).join(' · ')}</small>` : ''}</li>`).join('') || '<li>—</li>'}</ul></section>`;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- tasks
// v47.7 - The to-do list is a clean checklist: a tick box and the task text.
// Clicking the text opens the field where the task is filled in (TASK_TARGETS);
// orders and the prescription are buttons next to those fields (orderButton).
const firstEmpty = (selector) => {
  const all = [...document.querySelectorAll(selector)];
  return all.find((input) => !String(input.value || '').trim()) || all[0] || null;
};
const TASK_TARGETS = {
  'data:weight': () => '[data-visit="weight"]',
  'data:vitals': () => '[data-visit="bp"]',
  'data:fhr': () => '[data-visit="fhr"]',
  'data:uh': () => '[data-visit="uh"]',
  'data:fp': () => '[data-visit="fp"]',
  'own:urine': () => '[data-visit="proteinuria"]',
  'rx:iron': () => '[data-visit="fer"]',
  'data:education': () => '[data-visit="educationDone"]',
  'vaccine:tt': () => '[data-visit="legacyDetails.44"]',
  'own:hiv': () => '[data-visit="rapid.hiv"]',
  'own:syphilis': () => '[data-visit="rapid.syphilis"]',
  'own:hb': () => '[data-visit="rapid.hb"]',
  'own:hb-window': () => '[data-visit="rapid.hb"]',
  'data:anaemia': (task) => (task.visit === 1 ? '[data-path="history.anaemia.eyelid"]' : '[data-visit="rapid.hb"]'),
  'data:breast': () => '[data-path="history.mouth.breast"]',
  'data:mouth': () => '[data-path="history.mouth.stomatitis"]',
  'data:height': () => '[data-path="heightCm"]',
  'data:bmi': () => '[data-path="prePregnancyWeightKg"]',
  'order:HBSAG': () => firstEmpty('[data-path^="labs."][data-path$=".hbsag"]'),
  'order:BLOOD_GROUP': () => firstEmpty('[data-path^="labs."][data-path$=".group"]'),
  'order:OGTT': () => '[data-ask-doctor="OGTT"], [data-order="OGTT"]',
  'order:ULTRASOUND': () => firstEmpty('[data-path^="ultrasounds."][data-path$=".date"]'),
};
const TASK_GROUPS = [
  ['records', 'anc.cat.records', 'Records'], ['lab', 'anc.cat.lab', 'Laboratory'], ['exam', 'anc.cat.exam', 'Examination'],
  ['ultrasound', 'anc.cat.ultrasound', 'Ultrasound'], ['care', 'anc.cat.care', 'Care'],
];
// A task the data can complete: ticking it by hand means "not done here" (a reason is asked).
const hasData = (task) => !['check', 'auto:record'].includes(task.action) && task.action !== 'data:education';
function goToTask(task) {
  const target = TASK_TARGETS[task.action]?.(task);
  const node = typeof target === 'string' ? document.querySelector(target) : target;
  if (!node) return false;
  const section = node.closest('[data-anc-section]')?.dataset.ancSection;
  if (section) selectSection(section);
  node.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
  node.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  const place = node.closest('.anc-cell, td, .anc-yesno-row, label') || node;
  place.classList.remove('anc-flash'); void place.offsetWidth; place.classList.add('anc-flash');
  setTimeout(() => place.classList.remove('anc-flash'), 1800);
  return true;
}
// The order next to its field: the midwife orders it, or asks a doctor to order it.
function orderButton(code) {
  if (!canEdit()) return '';
  const allowed = code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? cfg.options.midwifeImaging.includes('ULTRASOUND') : cfg.options.midwifeLabTests.includes(code);
  return allowed ? `<button type="button" class="link anc-order no-print" data-order="${esc(code)}">${esc(code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? t('anc.orderUltrasound', 'Send to Imaging (pay at the Cashier)') : t('anc.orderLab', 'Send to the Laboratory (pay at the Cashier)'))}</button>`
    : `<button type="button" class="link anc-order no-print" data-ask-doctor="${esc(code)}">${esc(t('anc.askDoctor', 'Ask a doctor to order'))}</button>`;
}
function taskItem(task, overdue = false) {
  const state = task.state;
  const done = Boolean(state?.status === 'done' || task.autoDone);
  const closed = state?.status === 'closed';
  const text = TASK_TARGETS[task.action] ? `<a href="#" class="anc-task-text" data-task-go="${esc(task.key)}">${esc(task.text)}</a>`
    : `<label class="anc-task-text" for="anc-task-${esc(task.key)}">${esc(task.text)}</label>`;
  return `<li class="anc-task${done ? ' is-done' : ''}${closed ? ' is-closed' : ''}${overdue ? ' is-overdue' : ''}" data-task="${esc(task.key)}">
    <input type="checkbox" id="anc-task-${esc(task.key)}" data-task-done="${esc(task.key)}" aria-label="${esc(task.text)}"${done || closed ? ' checked' : ''}${task.autoDone ? ' data-auto="1" aria-readonly="true"' : ''}${canEdit() ? '' : ' disabled'}>
    <span class="anc-task-body">${text}${closed ? `<small>${esc(state.reason)}${state.reasonText ? ` · ${esc(state.reasonText)}` : ''}</small>` : ''}</span></li>`;
}
function taskGroups(tasks, overdue = false) {
  return TASK_GROUPS.map(([category, key, en]) => {
    const items = tasks.filter((task) => task.category === category);
    return items.length ? `<div class="anc-task-group"><h4>${esc(t(key, en))}</h4><ul class="anc-task-list">${items.map((task) => taskItem(task, overdue)).join('')}</ul></div>` : '';
  }).join('');
}
let shownTasks = new Map();
function renderTasks(status) {
  const host = $('anc-tasks');
  if (!data.pregnancy) { host.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  const header = status.scheduled ? `${t('anc.visitOf', 'Visit')} ${status.visit.visit}/${status.visitsPlanned} · ${status.visit.label}` : t('anc.notScheduled', 'Not a scheduled visit');
  const next = status.next ? `${t('anc.nextVisit', 'Next scheduled visit')}: ${status.next.label} · ${status.appointment}` : '';
  const stateOf = (task) => ({ ...task, state: record.tasks?.[task.key] || null });
  const current = status.tasks.map(stateOf), overdue = status.overdue.map(stateOf);
  shownTasks = new Map([...current, ...overdue].map((task) => [task.key, task]));
  const html = `
    <p class="anc-task-visit">${esc(header)}</p>${next ? `<p class="anc-task-next">${esc(next)}</p>` : ''}
    ${current.length ? `<div class="anc-task-groups">${taskGroups(current)}</div>` : ''}
    ${overdue.length ? `<h3 class="anc-overdue-title">${esc(t('anc.overdue', 'Overdue from earlier visits'))}</h3><div class="anc-task-groups">${taskGroups(overdue, true)}</div>` : ''}`;
  const wasClosed = host.querySelector('details')?.open === false;
  host.innerHTML = `<details class="anc-task-disclosure"${wasClosed ? '' : ' open'}><summary id="anc-tasks-title">${esc(t('anc.todo', 'To do at this visit'))} <span class="anc-task-count">${status.tasks.filter(task => task.autoDone || record.tasks?.[task.key]?.status === 'done').length}/${status.tasks.length}</span></summary><div>${html}</div></details>`;
  // v43.1: tasks stay inline; opening a patient never launches a blocking modal.
  tasksShown = true;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- render all
function renderMain() {
  const main = $('anc-main');
  if (!data.pregnancy) {
    main.innerHTML = pane('pregnancy', `<section class="anc-page"><p>${esc(t('anc.noRecord', 'No ANC record for this pregnancy yet.'))}</p>
      ${data.canEdit ? `<button type="button" data-action="open">${esc(t('anc.open', 'Open the ANC record'))}</button>` : ''}</section>`) + pane('legacy', legacySection());
    renderNavigation();
    return;
  }
  const status = currentStatus();
  main.innerHTML = `${data.pregnancy.status === 'open' ? pane('visit', visitForm(status)) : ''}${pane('pregnancy', page2())}
    ${pane('tests', `<section class="anc-page" data-page="3">${labTable()}${ultrasoundTable()}</section>`)}
    ${pane('care', supplementalVisit())}${pane('history', `<section class="anc-page" id="anc-visit-history">${visitTable()}</section>${historySection()}`)}
    ${pane('risk', chartSection(status))}${pane('legacy', legacySection())}`;
  const rapid = main.querySelector('.anc-rapid'), rx = main.querySelector('.anc-rx');
  if (rapid) main.querySelector('#anc-pane-tests > section').prepend(rapid);
  if (rx) main.querySelector('#anc-pane-care > section').append(rx);
  for (const [scope, path] of ANC_REQUIRED_FIELDS) {
    const input = main.querySelector(`[data-${scope === 'record' ? 'path' : 'visit'}="${path}"]`);
    if (!input) continue;
    input.required = true; input.setAttribute('aria-required', 'true');
    const marker = document.createElement('span'); marker.className = 'anc-required-mark'; marker.textContent = ' *'; marker.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    // v47.6: in a cell the star goes after the label text, so the boxes stay aligned.
    const cellLabel = input.closest('.anc-cell')?.querySelector('.anc-cell-label');
    if (cellLabel) cellLabel.append(marker); else input.before(marker);
  }
  renderNavigation();
}
function refreshLive() {
  if (!data?.pregnancy) { renderTop(null); return; }
  const status = currentStatus();
  renderTop(status);
  renderSummary(status);
  renderTasks(status);
  refreshChart(status);
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-anc-ga]').forEach(node => { node.textContent = status.ga?.label || ''; });
  document.querySelectorAll('.anc-visit-name').forEach((node) => { node.textContent = status.scheduled ? `${t('anc.visitOf', 'Visit')} ${status.visit.visit}/${status.visitsPlanned} · ${status.visit.label}` : t('anc.notScheduled', 'Not a scheduled visit'); });
}
function renderAll() { renderMain(); refreshLive(); }

// Today's visit starts from the saved visit of today, or from Triage.
function startDraft() {
  const saved = (data.visits || []).find((visit) => visit.date === data.today && visit.patientKey === patientKey);
  const imported = data.imported || {};
  draft = saved ? { ...saved } : { weight: imported.weightKg, bp: imported.bp, pulse: imported.pulse, temperature: imported.temperature, spo2: imported.spo2, rapid: {}, imported };
  if (!draft.rapid) draft.rapid = {};
  // Weight before pregnancy: an empty field takes the Triage weight (v47.4: at any GA
  // when the owner setting is null; with a number, only at a first visit before it).
  // The midwife can type the real weight before pregnancy; BMI follows.
  const status = data.status;
  const maxWeeks = cfg.options.prePregnancyWeightFromTriageMaxWeeks;
  const weightAllowed = maxWeeks == null || (status?.ga && status.ga.weeks < maxWeeks && !(data.visits || []).length);
  if (record && !record.prePregnancyWeightKg && imported.weightKg && weightAllowed) {
    record.prePregnancyWeightKg = imported.weightKg;
    record.prePregnancyWeightSource = 'triage';
  }
  if (record && !record.heightCm && imported.heightCm) record.heightCm = imported.heightCm;
  if (!draft.appointment && status?.appointment) draft.appointment = status.appointment;
}

async function load() {
  try {
    if (!cfg) cfg = await api('/api/maternal/config');
    data = await api(`/api/anc/record?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}`);
    record = data.pregnancy ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.pregnancy.record || {})) : null;
    if (record) startDraft();
    renderAll();
    setStatus('');
  } catch (error) { setStatus(error.message, true); }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- events
// Boxes ticked from the patient data (colour chart, v47.7 to-do list) cannot be
// unticked (not disabled, so the tick keeps its colour); the data is corrected instead.
document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
  if (event.target.matches?.('.anc-chart input[data-auto], #anc-tasks input[data-auto]')) event.preventDefault();
});
document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
  const target = event.target;
  if (target.dataset.path && record) {
    setPath(record, target.dataset.path, target.type === 'checkbox' ? target.checked : target.value);
    if (target.dataset.path === 'prePregnancyWeightKg') record.prePregnancyWeightSource = 'typed';
    refreshLive();
  } else if (target.dataset.visit) {
    setPath(draft, target.dataset.visit, target.type === 'checkbox' ? target.checked : target.value);
    refreshLive();
  } else if (target.dataset.rx !== undefined) {
    prescription[Number(target.dataset.rx)][target.dataset.rxKey] = target.value;
  }
});
document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
  const target = event.target;
  if (target.type === 'radio' && target.dataset.path && record) { setPath(record, target.dataset.path, target.value); refreshLive(); }
  if (target.tagName === 'SELECT' && target.dataset.path && record) { setPath(record, target.dataset.path, target.value); refreshLive(); }
  if (target.tagName === 'SELECT' && target.dataset.visit) { setPath(draft, target.dataset.visit, target.value); refreshLive(); }
  if (target.tagName === 'SELECT' && target.dataset.rx !== undefined) { prescription[Number(target.dataset.rx)][target.dataset.rxKey] = target.value; renderMain(); }
});

const body = (extra = {}) => ({ patientKey, ...extra });
let busy = false;
async function run(action) {
  if (busy) return;
  busy = true;
  const buttons = [...document.querySelectorAll('.anc-actions button:not(:disabled)')];
  buttons.forEach(button => { button.disabled = true; });
  setStatus('…');
  try { const result = await action(); setStatus(result === false ? '' : t('anc.saved', 'Saved.')); } catch (error) { setStatus(error.message, true); }
  finally { busy = false; buttons.forEach(button => { button.disabled = false; }); }
}

function addLine(item) {
  if (!item) return;
  const unit = window.BorkeoMedicine?.optionUnit?.(item) || item.unit;
  prescription.push({ name: item.label, cmsCode: item.code || '', unit, route: 'po', doseAmount: '1', scheduleType: 'qd_m', d: '30', m: '', a: '', e: '', n: '', note: '' });
  renderMain();
}

function closeDialog(event) {
  const dialog = event.target.closest('dialog');
  dialog?.close();
}

function askCloseReason(key) {
  const dialog = $('anc-close-dialog');
  const reasons = cfg.options.taskCloseReasons;
  dialog.innerHTML = `<form method="dialog" class="anc-dialog-body"><h3>${esc(t('anc.closeUndone', 'Close without doing'))}</h3>
    ${reasons.map((reason, index) => `<label class="anc-choice"><input type="radio" name="anc-close-reason" value="${esc(reason)}"${index === 0 ? ' checked' : ''}> ${esc(reason)}</label>`).join('<br>')}
    <p><input type="text" class="anc-field wide" name="anc-close-text" maxlength="500" placeholder="${esc(t('anc.reason', 'Reason'))}"></p>
    <p class="anc-error" role="status"></p>
    <div class="anc-actions"><button type="button" class="secondary" data-dialog-close>${esc(t('drugSafety.cancel', 'Cancel'))}</button><button type="submit">${esc(t('drugSafety.save', 'Save'))}</button></div></form>`;
  dialog.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const reason = dialog.querySelector('input[name="anc-close-reason"]:checked')?.value || '';
    const reasonText = dialog.querySelector('input[name="anc-close-text"]').value.trim();
    try {
      const result = await api(`/api/anc/record/${data.pregnancy.id}/task`, body({ key, action: 'close', reason, reasonText }));
      record.tasks = result.pregnancy.record.tasks;
      data.pregnancy = result.pregnancy;
      dialog.close();
      refreshLive();
    } catch (error) { dialog.querySelector('.anc-error').textContent = error.message; }
  });
  dialog.addEventListener('close', () => refreshLive(), { once: true });
  dialog.showModal();
}

document.addEventListener('click', async (event) => {
  const button = event.target.closest('button, a.link, a[data-task-go]');
  if (!button) return;
  if (button.dataset.ancNav) { selectSection(button.dataset.ancNav); return; }
  if (button.hasAttribute('data-dialog-close')) { closeDialog(event); return; }
  const action = button.dataset.action;
  const id = data?.pregnancy?.id;
  if (action === 'open') return run(async () => { await api('/api/anc/record/open', body()); await load(); });
  if (action === 'save-record') return run(async () => {
    const result = await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/save`, body({ record, expectedVersion: data.pregnancy.version }));
    data.pregnancy = result.pregnancy; data.colourHistory = result.colourHistory; data.status = result.status;
    record = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result.pregnancy.record)); renderAll();
  });
  if (action === 'save-visit') return run(async () => {
    const missing = missingAncFields(record, draft);
    if (missing.length) {
      const [scope, path] = missing[0];
      const input = document.querySelector(`[data-${scope === 'record' ? 'path' : 'visit'}="${path}"]`);
      const pane = input?.closest('[data-anc-section]');
      if (pane) selectSection(pane.dataset.ancSection);
      window.BorkeoRequiredFields?.validate(pane || $('anc-main'));
      input?.focus();
      return false;
    }
    const status = currentStatus();
    const visit = { ...draft, wp: weightGain(draft.weight), notScheduled: !status.scheduled };
    delete visit.id;
    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/save-all`, body({ record, visit, expectedVersion: data.pregnancy.version }));
    await load();
  });
  if (action === 'print') { printRecord(); return; }
  if (action === 'print-referral') { printReferral(); return; }
  if (action === 'referral') return run(async () => {
    const treatment = window.prompt(t('anc.treatmentGiven', 'Treatment given before the referral')) ?? '';
    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/referral`, body({ treatment }));
    await load();
  });
  if (action === 'sign') return run(async () => { await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/sign`, body()); await load(); });
  if (action === 'close') return run(async () => {
    const delivered = window.confirm(t('anc.closeDelivered', 'Has the woman delivered? OK = delivered, Cancel = close for another reason'));
    const reason = delivered ? '' : (window.prompt(t('anc.reason', 'Reason')) || '');
    if (!delivered && !reason) return;
    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/close`, body({ status: delivered ? 'delivered' : 'closed', reason }));
    await load();
  });
  if (action === 'rx-add') { addLine((cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).find((item) => String(item.id) === $('anc-rx-pick').value)); return; }
  if (action === 'rx-iron') {
    const name = cfg.options.ironPrescriptionMedicine;
    addLine((cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).find((item) => item.name === name && /tablet/i.test(item.form)) || (cfg.midwifeMedicines || []).find((item) => item.name === name));
    selectSection('care');
    document.querySelector('.anc-rx')?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
    return;
  }
  if (button.dataset.rxRemove !== undefined) { prescription.splice(Number(button.dataset.rxRemove), 1); renderMain(); return; }
  if (action === 'rx-send') return run(async () => {
    const medications = prescription.map((line) => ({ ...line, total: rxTotal(line) }));
    await api(`/api/anc/record/${id}/prescription`, body({ medications }));
    const tablets = medications.filter((line) => /ferrous|folic/i.test(line.name)).reduce((sum, line) => sum + Number(line.total || 0), 0);
    if (tablets) draft.fer = String(tablets);
    prescription = [];
    renderMain(); refreshLive();
  });
  if (button.dataset.order) return run(async () => {
    const code = button.dataset.order;
    const request = code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? { clinicalInfo: 'ANC', diagnosis: 'ANC' } : { items: [{ code }], clinicalInfo: 'ANC' };
    await api('/api/paraclinic/orders', body({ modality: code === 'ULTRASOUND' ? 'ULTRASOUND' : 'LAB', request, chargeMode: 'PAID', handoffMode: 'DIRECT' }));
    setStatus(t('anc.ordered', 'Sent. The patient pays at the Cashier.'));
  });
  if (button.dataset.askDoctor) return run(async () => { await api('/api/maternal/ask-doctor', body({ test: button.dataset.askDoctor })); });
  if (button.dataset.taskGo) { event.preventDefault(); const task = shownTasks.get(button.dataset.taskGo); if (task) goToTask(task); return; }
});
document.addEventListener('change', async (event) => {
  const key = event.target.dataset.taskDone;
  if (!key) return;
  const task = shownTasks.get(key);
  if (event.target.checked && task && !task.state) {
    if (task.action === 'data:education') {
      draft.educationDone = true;
      const box = document.querySelector('[data-visit="educationDone"]');
      if (box) box.checked = true;
      refreshLive();
      return;
    }
    if (hasData(task)) { event.target.checked = false; askCloseReason(key); return; }
  }
  try {
    const result = await api(`/api/anc/record/${data.pregnancy.id}/task`, body({ key, action: event.target.checked ? 'done' : 'reopen' }));
    record.tasks = result.pregnancy.record.tasks;
    refreshLive();
  } catch (error) { setStatus(error.message, true); }
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- print
function printRecord() {
  const status = currentStatus();
  const header = `<header class="anc-print-head"><img src="/public/logo-hospital.jpg" alt=""><h1>${esc(L().title)}</h1>
    <div class="anc-colour-boxes">${['green', 'yellow', 'red'].map((key) => `<span class="anc-colour-box anc-box-${key}"><span class="anc-box">${status.colour === key ? '✓' : ''}</span>${esc(colourWord(key))}</span>`).join('')}</div></header>`;
  const footer = `<footer class="anc-print-foot">${esc(L().footer)}</footer>`;
  const snapshot = $('anc-main').cloneNode(true);
  snapshot.querySelectorAll('.anc-visit, .anc-chart, .anc-history, .anc-hint, .anc-rapid, .anc-order, .anc-order-line').forEach((node) => node.remove());
  const visitHistory = snapshot.querySelector('#anc-visit-history .anc-box-panel');
  if (visitHistory) snapshot.querySelector('[data-page="3"]')?.append(visitHistory);
  snapshot.querySelectorAll('input').forEach((input) => {
    const span = document.createElement('span');
    span.className = 'anc-print-value';
    span.textContent = input.type === 'checkbox' || input.type === 'radio' ? (input.checked ? '☑' : '☐') : input.value;
    input.replaceWith(span);
  });
  snapshot.querySelectorAll('select').forEach((select) => { const span = document.createElement('span'); span.textContent = select.selectedOptions[0]?.textContent || ''; select.replaceWith(span); });
  const pages = Array.from(snapshot.querySelectorAll('.anc-page[data-page]')).map((page) => `<div class="anc-print-page">${header}${page.innerHTML}${footer}</div>`).join('');
  $('anc-print').innerHTML = pages;
  document.body.classList.add('anc-printing');
  window.addEventListener('afterprint', () => document.body.classList.remove('anc-printing'), { once: true });
  window.print();
}

function printReferral() {
  const visit = [...(data.visits || [])].reverse().find((item) => item.referral?.signed);
  if (!visit) return;
  const status = currentStatus();
  $('anc-print').innerHTML = referralLetterHtml({ data, record, visit, status, labels: L(), t, esc });
  document.body.classList.add('anc-printing', 'anc-printing-letter');
  window.addEventListener('afterprint', () => document.body.classList.remove('anc-printing', 'anc-printing-letter'), { once: true });
  window.print();
}

window.addEventListener('borkeo-language-change', () => data && renderAll());
document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
  if (!event.target.matches('[data-anc-nav]') || !['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'Home', 'End'].includes(event.key)) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  const tabs = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-anc-nav]')], index = tabs.indexOf(event.target);
  const next = event.key === 'Home' ? 0 : event.key === 'End' ? tabs.length - 1 : (index + (event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : tabs.length - 1)) % tabs.length;
  selectSection(tabs[next].dataset.ancNav, true);
});
load();

  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(projectRoot,'package.json'),'utf8')).version.match(/^\d+\.\d+/)?.[0] || '22.2'; }
  catch { return '22.2'; }

const clinicalFormTypes = new Set(CLINICAL_FORM_TYPES);
const clinicalWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormReadRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD']);

function landingPathForRole(role, wardCode = '') {
  role = String(role || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role)) return '/billing';
  if (role === 'LAB') return '/lab';
  if (role === 'IMAGING') return '/imaging';
  if (role === 'TRIAGE') return '/triage';
  if (role === 'REGISTRATION') return '/emr?view=register';
  if (role === 'NURSE' && String(wardCode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd') return '/opd-paraclinic-nurse';
  if (role === 'WARD_HEAD' || role === 'CHIEF_WARD') return '/ward-medicines';
  if (role === 'NURSE' || role === 'MIDWIFE') return '/departments';
  return '/home';
}

function messagingVisibility(session) {
  return { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session), username: session.username };
}

function messagingRoleAllowed(role) {
  return new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','REGISTRATION','TRIAGE','DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CHIEF_WARD','WARD_HEAD','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY']).has(String(role || '').toUpperCase());
}

function destinationExists(type, code='') {
  type = String(type || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(type)) return true;
  if (type !== 'WARD') return false;
  return WARDS.some(ward => ward.code === String(code || '').toLowerCase());
}

function automaticWardMessage(database, actor, patient, subject, body, options = {}) {
  const wardCode = String(options.wardCode || patientWorkWard(patient) || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!wardCode) return null;
  return createSystemMessage(database, {
    senderUserId: actor.userId,
    senderRole: actor.role,
    senderWardCode: actor.wardCode || '',
    destinationType: 'WARD',
    destinationCode: wardCode,
    destinationRole: options.destinationRole || '',
    subject,
    body,
    patientKey: patient.patient_key,
  });
}

function notifyOpdDoctorsPaymentCompleted(database, session, patient, bill, payments = []) {
  if (String(patient?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() !== 'opd') return null;
  const money = (amount, currency) => currency === 'USD'
    ? `$${Number(amount || 0).toFixed(2)}`
    : `${Math.round(Number(amount || 0)).toLocaleString('en-US')} ៛`;
  const paid = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.paid || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ') || payments.map(item => money(item.amount, item.currency)).join(' + ');
  const waived = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.waived || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
  const settlement = paid || (waived ? `Fully waived: ${waived}` : 'Invoice settled');
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const invoice = bill?.invoice?.invoiceNo || bill?.invoice?.id || '—';
  return automaticWardMessage(database,
    { userId:session.user_id, role:sessionRole(session), wardCode:sessionWard(session) },
    patient,
    'Cashier payment completed',
    `${name} · invoice ${invoice} · ${settlement}. The OPD invoice is settled; the patient may continue with the OPD doctor.`,
    { wardCode:'opd', destinationRole:'DOCTOR' });
}

function admissionWardCode(update) {
  const room = String(update?.roomCode || '').toLowerCase();
  const department = String(update?.departmentCode || '').toLowerCase();
  if (room === 'medical') return 'medicine';
  if (room === 'surgical') return 'surgery';
  if (['emergency','pediatric','gynecology','obstetric','tb','infectious','icu','ncu'].includes(room)) return room;
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') return room === 'obstetric' ? 'obstetric' : 'gynecology';
  return department;
}

function notifyTriageReferral(database, session, patient) {
  const department = String(patient.department_code || '').toLowerCase();
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const actor = { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session) };
  const getWardKh = (code) => {
    try {
      const w = database.findWard?.(code);
      if (w?.nameKh) return w.nameKh;
    } catch (_) {}
    const map = {
      medicine: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺទូទៅ',
      surgery: 'ផ្នែកវះកាត់',
      pediatric: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      pediatrics: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      maternity: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      obstetric: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      gynecology: 'ផ្នែករោគស្ត្រី',
      emergency: 'ផ្នែកសង្គ្រោះបន្ទាន់',
      tb: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺរបេង',
      infectious: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺឆ្លង',
      dental: 'ផ្នែកធ្មេញ',
      mental: 'ផ្នែកសុខភាពផ្លូវចិត្ត',
      opd: 'ផ្នែកពិគ្រោះក្រៅ'
    };
    return map[code] || (code ? `ផ្នែក ${code}` : 'ផ្នែកសម្រាកពេទ្យ');
  };
  const send = (wardCode, destinationRole) => {
    const targetDept = getWardKh(wardCode);
    const msg = `អ្នកជំងឺឈ្មោះ ${name} ត្រូវបានបញ្ជូនពីផ្នែកទ្រីយ៉ាមកផ្នែក ${targetDept.replace(/^ផ្នែក\s*/, '')} សូមពិនិត្យមើល និងរៀបចំជូនអ្នកជំងឺ`;
    return automaticWardMessage(
      database, actor, patient,
      msg,
      msg,
      { wardCode, destinationRole },
    );
  };
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') {
    send('gynecology', 'NURSE');
    send('obstetric', 'MIDWIFE');
    return;
  }
  const wardCode = patientWorkWard(patient) || department;
  if (!wardCode || wardCode === 'opd') return;
  send(wardCode, wardCode === 'obstetric' ? 'MIDWIFE' : 'NURSE');
}

// v42.4: behind a hosting proxy (TRUST_PROXY=true) the client address is the
// last address in X-Forwarded-For (added by the proxy itself; earlier entries
// can be sent by the client). Login lockout, the audit log and the record view
// log all use this address. Without TRUST_PROXY the header is ignored.
function requestIp(request) {
  if (request.borkeoTrustProxy) {
    const forwarded = String(request.headers['x-forwarded-for'] || '').split(',').map(part => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (forwarded.length) return forwarded[forwarded.length - 1];
  }
  return request.socket.remoteAddress ?? 'unknown';
}

const htmlTemplateCache = new Map();
const staticFileCache = new Map();
const staticCompressedCache = new Map();

function compressIfNeeded(request, response, buffer, contentType) {
  if (!buffer || buffer.length < 512) return buffer;
  const isCompressible = /^(text\/|application\/json|application\/javascript|font\/ttf|image\/svg\+xml)/i.test(contentType);
  if (!isCompressible) return buffer;

  const accept = String(request?.headers?.['accept-encoding'] || '');
  if (/\bgzip\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'gzip');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.gzipSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  if (/\bdeflate\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'deflate');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.deflateSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  return buffer;
}

function getProcessedHtml(filePath, contentType, security, APP_VERSION) {
  const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
  const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${stat.mtimeMs}:${APP_VERSION}:${security?.requestPath || ''}`;
  const cached = htmlTemplateCache.get(filePath);
  if (cached && cached.cacheKey === cacheKey) {
    return cached.bytes;
  }
  let html = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
  html = html.replace(/(<span\b[^>]*\bdata-app-version\b[^>]*>)[^<]*(<\/span>)/g, (_, open, close) => `${open}v${APP_VERSION}${close}`);
  html = html.replace(/Borkeo EMR v\d+\.\d+/g, `Borkeo EMR v${APP_VERSION}`);
  html = html.replace(/<head\b[^>]*>/i, head => head + '<style>.emr-language-switch, [data-emr-language], #langBtn, .lang-btn, body #emr-site-banner .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, #emr-session-control .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch { display: none !important; visibility: hidden !important; opacity: 0 !important; pointer-events: none !important; width: 0 !important; height: 0 !important; max-width: 0 !important; max-height: 0 !important; margin: 0 !important; padding: 0 !important; border: 0 !important; position: absolute !important; left: -9999px !important; clip: rect(0,0,0,0) !important; }</style><script src="/public/emr-language-lock.js?v=3760"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-required-fields.css?v=3770"><script src="/public/emr-required-fields.js?v=3770"></script>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/design-system.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/components.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/app-shell.css?v=3980"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/patient-banner.css?v=4100"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/soap-note.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/clinical-data.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/worklists-feedback.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/interface-theme.css?v=1970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/npca.css?v=4280"><script src="/public/npca-widget.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/npca-forms.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/components.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/nav-config.js?v=3980" defer></script><script src="/public/app-shell.js?v=3980" defer></script><script src="/public/patient-banner.js?v=4170" defer></script><script src="/public/soap-note.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/clinical-data.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/worklists-feedback.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/interface-theme.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/shared-controls.css?v=1970"><script src="/public/request-badges.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  // v42.3: one medication unit table for every page (old Khmer unit labels shown as mg, mL, mcg, g, IU).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/record-access.js?v=4570" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medicine-units.js?v=4230"></script></head>');
  // v42.5: one medication route table for every page (new lines: IV, IM, SC and the simple routes).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medication-routes.js?v=4250"></script></head>');
  // v42.3: medicine name search in the prescription forms (medical record page only).
