      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/') {
        if (!session) return redirect(response, '/login', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
        return redirect(response, identity.needsContext ? '/context' : landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }


      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/health') {
        return sendJson(response, 200, { status: 'ok' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/login') {
        // An account that must change its password stays on /login, where the
        // change-password form is shown; redirecting it would loop (v39.9).
        if (session && session.must_change_password !== 1) {
          const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
          return redirect(response, identity.needsContext ? '/context' : landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.publicDirectory, 'login.html'), 'text/html; charset=utf-8', {
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && publicFiles.has(pathname)) {
        const [filename, contentType] = publicFiles.get(pathname);
        const isStaticFontOrImage = contentType.startsWith('font/') || contentType.startsWith('image/');
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.publicDirectory, filename), contentType, {
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        }, isStaticFontOrImage);
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && (pathname.startsWith('/data/gazetteer/') || pathname.startsWith('/public/data/gazetteer/'))) {
        const prefix = pathname.startsWith('/public/data/gazetteer/') ? '/public/data/gazetteer/' : '/data/gazetteer/';
        const relative = pathname.slice(prefix.length);
        if (!relative || relative.includes('..')) {
          return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Invalid path.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        const filePath = path.join(projectRoot, 'data', 'gazetteer', relative);
        if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
          const contentType = relative.endsWith('.json') ? 'application/json; charset=utf-8' : 'text/plain; charset=utf-8';
          return sendFile(response, filePath, contentType, {
            secure: config.cookieSecure,
          }, true);
        }
        return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Gazetteer data not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }

      if (request.method === 'GET' && protectedPages.has(pathname)) {
        if (!session) return redirect(response, '/login', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
        if (identity.needsContext && pathname !== '/context') {
          return redirect(response, '/context', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/home' && ['LAB','IMAGING','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase())) {
          return redirect(response, landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/emr' && ['CLERK', 'ACCOUNTANT'].includes(String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase())) {
          const patientKey = url.searchParams.get('patient') || url.searchParams.get('patientKey') || '';
          const target = patientKey ? `/billing?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}&history=1` : '/billing';
          return redirect(response, target, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/emr' && String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRIAGE') {
          const requestedView = url.searchParams.get('view');
          if (requestedView !== 'triage') {
            url.searchParams.set('view', 'triage');
            return redirect(response, `${url.pathname}?${url.searchParams.toString()}`, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
          }
        }
        if(pathname==='/emr' && url.searchParams.get('patient') && !RECORD_MANAGEMENT.includes(identity.activeRole)){
          const row=database.findPatient(url.searchParams.get('patient'));
          if(row){
            const policy=encounterAccess(database,session,row);
            if(!policy.canView)return sendJson(response,403,{error:'អ្នកមិនមានសិទ្ធិមើលកំណត់ត្រានេះទេ។'},{secure:config.cookieSecure});
            const requested=url.searchParams.get('view')||'';
            const allowed=requested===policy.view || policy.view==='medical'&&['daily','discharge'].includes(requested) || policy.view==='opd'&&requested==='opd-paraclinic';
            if(!allowed){url.searchParams.set('view',policy.view);return redirect(response,url.pathname+'?'+url.searchParams.toString(),{secure:config.cookieSecure});}
          }
        }
        if (pathname !== '/context' && !roleCanPage(identity.activeRole, pathname)) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Your active role cannot open this page.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/ipd-transfer' && (identity.activeRole !== 'DOCTOR' || identity.activeWard?.code !== 'opd')) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'The IPD transfer workspace is available to OPD doctors only.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/opd-paraclinic-nurse' && (identity.activeRole !== 'NURSE' || identity.activeWard?.code !== 'opd')) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'The OPD paraclinic handoff queue is available to OPD nurses only.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        const [filename, legacy] = protectedPages.get(pathname);
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.protectedDirectory, filename), 'text/html; charset=utf-8', {
          legacy,
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
          requestPath: pathname,
        });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname.startsWith('/emr-assets/')) {
        if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const assetName = pathname.slice('/emr-assets/'.length);
        if (!emrAssets.has(assetName)) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const [filename, contentType] = emrAssets.get(assetName);
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.protectedDirectory, 'assets', filename), contentType, {
          legacy: true,
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        }, true);
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/favicon.ico') {
        applySecurityHeaders(response, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        response.writeHead(204);
        return response.end();
      }
      return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    } catch (error) {
      const status = Number.isInteger(error.status) ? error.status : 500;
      if (status >= 500) {
        reqLogger.error(`Unhandled error on ${request.method} ${pathname}: ${error.message}`, { error, status });
      } else if (status >= 400) {
        reqLogger.warn(`Client error on ${request.method} ${pathname}: ${error.message}`, { status });
      }
      return sendJson(response, status, {
        error: status === 500 ? 'The server could not complete the request.' : error.message,
      }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
  });

  server.requestTimeout = 30_000;
  server.headersTimeout = 15_000;
  server.keepAliveTimeout = 5_000;
  server.once('listening', () => npca.start());
  server.on('close', () => npca.stop());
  server.on('close', () => diagnosisSearch.close());
  return {
    server,
    database,
    config,
    persistence,
    recovery,
    npca,
    access: {
      getUserImagingSubtypes: (userId) => getUserImagingSubtypes(database, userId),
      setUserImagingSubtypes: (userId, subtypes, actorId) => setUserImagingSubtypes(database, userId, subtypes, actorId),
    }
  };
}

export async function startServer() {
  const emrMode = (process.env.EMR_MODE || '').toLowerCase();
  const demoMode = emrMode === 'demo';
  const host = process.env.HOST || '127.0.0.1';
  const application = createEmrServer({ demoMode, host });
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    application.server.once('error', reject);
    application.server.listen(application.config.port, application.config.host, resolve);
  });
  const modeLabel = application.config.demoMode ? 'DEMO (username-only)' : 'PRODUCTION (password required)';
  console.log(`Borkeo EMR v${APP_VERSION} [${modeLabel}] listening on http://${application.config.host}:${application.config.port}`);
  if (application.config.host === '0.0.0.0') {
    try {
      const nets = os.networkInterfaces();
      for (const name of Object.keys(nets)) {
        for (const net of nets[name] || []) {
          if (net.family === 'IPv4' && !net.internal && !net.address.startsWith('169.254')) {
            console.log(`Mobile / Phone access (Same Wi-Fi): http://${net.address}:${application.config.port}`);
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  } else {
    console.log(`Listening on local loopback address ${application.config.host} only (not exposed to network).`);
  }
  if (application.config.demoMode) {
    console.log(`DEMO: username-only accounts, localhost only. Demo data: ${application.config.databasePath}`);
    console.log('Demo usernames: admin, registration, triage, doctor_medicine, pharmacy.');
  }
  if (application.config.dataDirectory) console.log(`Persistent EMR data (DATA_DIR): ${application.config.dataDirectory}`);
  if (application.config.autoDatabase) {
    console.log(`Persistent EMR data: ${application.config.databasePath}`);
    if (application.persistence.sourcePath) console.log(`Initial database recovered from: ${application.persistence.sourcePath}`);
    if (application.recovery.patients || application.recovery.forms) console.log(`Recovered ${application.recovery.patients} patient record(s) and ${application.recovery.forms} clinical form(s) from ${application.recovery.sources} older database(s).`);
  }

  const cleanup = setInterval(() => application.database.deleteExpiredSessions(Date.now()), 30 * 60_000);
  cleanup.unref();
  // v41.6: unacknowledged critical results go to the ward doctors and ward head (every minute).
  const escalation = setInterval(() => {
    try { escalateCriticalResults(application.database, clinicalOptions().criticalEscalation); } catch (error) { console.error('Critical result escalation failed:', error.message); }
  }, 60_000);
  escalation.unref();
  const backupScheduler = startBackupScheduler(application.database, application.config, { backupDirectory: application.config.backupDirectory || undefined });
  const stop = () => application.server.close(() => {
    backupScheduler.stop();
    application.database.close();
    process.exit(0);
  });
  process.once('SIGINT', stop);
  process.once('SIGTERM', stop);
  return application;
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === path.resolve(currentFile)) {
  startServer().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
  });
}

export { Logger, defaultLogger, checkDatabaseIntegrity, startBackupScheduler };

===== END FILE: server/server.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/v426-midwife-anc-browser.mjs =====
// v42.6 browser check (explicit, like tests/v425-medicine-list-browser.mjs; not part of npm test):
//   node --test tests/v426-midwife-anc-browser.mjs
// Synthetic patients only, in-memory database: green, yellow and red pregnancies
// across visits, a vaccination, a cervical screening and a safe abortion.
import test from 'node:test';
import { completeAncRequired } from './helpers/anc-complete.mjs';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { createEmrServer } from '../server/server.mjs';
import { opdLocalDate } from '../server/opd-queue.mjs';

const sleep = ms => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function openBrowser() {
  const executable = [process.env.EMR_TEST_BROWSER, 'C:/Program Files (x86)/Microsoft/Edge/Application/msedge.exe', 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe']
    .find(file => file && fs.existsSync(file));
  assert.ok(executable, 'Set EMR_TEST_BROWSER to run this explicit Chromium test.');
  const debugPort = 9400 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 400);
  const profile = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'borkeo-v426-'));
  const child = spawn(executable, ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run', `--remote-debugging-port=${debugPort}`, `--user-data-dir=${profile}`, 'about:blank'], { stdio: 'ignore' });
  let url;
  for (let i = 0; i < 80 && !url; i++) {
    try { url = (await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${debugPort}/json/list`)).json()).find(item => item.type === 'page')?.webSocketDebuggerUrl; } catch {}
    if (!url) await sleep(250);
  }
  const ws = new WebSocket(url);
  await new Promise(resolve => ws.addEventListener('open', resolve, { once: true }));
  let seq = 0;
  const pending = new Map();
  const state = { loads: 0, errors: [] };
  ws.addEventListener('message', event => {
    const message = JSON.parse(event.data);
    if (message.id && pending.has(message.id)) { pending.get(message.id)(message); pending.delete(message.id); return; }
    if (message.method === 'Page.loadEventFired') state.loads++;
    if (message.method === 'Runtime.exceptionThrown') state.errors.push(message.params.exceptionDetails?.exception?.description || message.params.exceptionDetails?.text);
    if (message.method === 'Page.javascriptDialogOpening') send('Page.handleJavaScriptDialog', { accept: true }).catch(() => {});
  });
  const send = (method, params = {}) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const id = ++seq;
    pending.set(id, message => message.error ? reject(new Error(`${method}: ${message.error.message}`)) : resolve(message.result));
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }));
  });
  await send('Page.enable'); await send('Runtime.enable'); await send('Network.enable');
  await send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 1440, height: 900, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: false });
  const evaluate = async expression => {
    const result = await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression, awaitPromise: true, returnByValue: true });
    if (result.exceptionDetails) throw new Error(result.exceptionDetails.exception?.description || result.exceptionDetails.text);
    return result.result.value;
  };
  const waitFor = async (expression, label, timeout = 20000) => {
    const until = Date.now() + timeout;
    while (Date.now() < until) { try { if (await evaluate(expression)) return; } catch {} await sleep(150); }
    throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${label}`);
  };
  const key = async (keyName, code) => {
    const keyCode = { ArrowDown: 40, ArrowUp: 38, Enter: 13, Escape: 27, Backspace: 8 }[keyName];
    await send('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'rawKeyDown', key: keyName, code: code || keyName, windowsVirtualKeyCode: keyCode });
    await send('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'keyUp', key: keyName, code: code || keyName, windowsVirtualKeyCode: keyCode });
  };
  const type = text => send('Input.insertText', { text });
  const close = () => { ws.close(); child.kill(); };
  return { send, evaluate, waitFor, key, type, close, state };
}

test('v42.6 midwife services in the browser: green, yellow and red pregnancies, vaccination, cervical screening, safe abortion', { timeout: 400000 }, async () => {
  const app = createEmrServer({ demoMode: true, databasePath: ':memory:', host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, appOrigin: 'http://127.0.0.1:0', nodeEnv: 'test' });
  await new Promise(resolve => app.server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  const origin = `http://127.0.0.1:${app.server.address().port}`;
  app.config.appOrigin = origin;
  const browser = await openBrowser();
  const today = opdLocalDate();
  const login = async (username) => {
    const response = await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { Origin: origin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ username }) });
    const Cookie = response.headers.get('set-cookie').split(';')[0];
    const me = await (await fetch(origin + '/api/auth/me', { headers: { Cookie } })).json();
    return { Cookie, Origin: origin, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': me.csrfToken };
  };
  const call = async (headers, method, url, body) => {
    const response = await fetch(origin + url, { method, headers, body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body) });
    return { status: response.status, body: await response.json().catch(() => ({})) };
  };
  const staff = {};
  for (const name of ['registration', 'triage', 'clerk', 'nurse_obstetric', 'doctor_obstetric', 'doctor_gynecology']) staff[name] = await login(name);
  const visit = async (caseNumber, service, { healthId = `H-${caseNumber}`, age = '25', previous = '', sex = 'ស្រី' } = {}) => {
    const key = `case:${caseNumber}`;
    const summary = { caseNumber, healthId, firstName: 'Synthetic', lastName: caseNumber, fullName: `Synthetic ${caseNumber}`, sex, age, dateOfBirth: '', phone: '', equityCard: '', paymentType: 'CASH', departmentCode: '', departmentNameKh: '', departmentNameEn: '', serviceMode: '', entryDate: today, dischargeDate: '' };
    let response = await call(staff.registration, 'PUT', '/api/patient-record', previous ? { patientKey: key, summary, newVisitFrom: previous, data: { 'scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_old': true } } : { patientKey: key, summary, data: { 'scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_new': true } });
    assert.equal(response.status, 200, JSON.stringify(response.body));
    const version = (await call(staff.registration, 'GET', `/api/patient-record?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(key)}`)).body.patient.version;
    const values = { p2_weight: '52', p2_height: '158', p2_bp_sys: '110', p2_bp_dia: '70', p2_pulse: '80', p2_temp: '36.8', p2_spo2: '98', p2_resp: '18' };
    response = await call(staff.triage, 'PUT', '/api/patient-record', { patientKey: key, expectedVersion: version, triageSave: true,
      summary: { ...summary, departmentCode: service, departmentNameKh: service, departmentNameEn: service, serviceMode: 'opd' },
      data: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(values).map(([k, v]) => [`scope:triage:id:${k}`, v])) });
    assert.equal(response.status, 200, JSON.stringify(response.body));
    if (service !== 'vaccination') {
      const bill = (await call(staff.clerk, 'GET', `/api/finance/patient?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(key)}`)).body.bill;
      assert.equal((await call(staff.clerk, 'POST', '/api/finance/payments', { patientKey: key, invoiceId: bill.invoice.id, source: 'CASH', amount: bill.summary.KHR.balance, currency: 'KHR' })).status, 201);
    }
    return key;
  };
  const useAccount = async (username) => {
    const [name, value] = staff[username].Cookie.split('=');
    await browser.send('Network.clearBrowserCookies');
    await browser.send('Network.setCookie', { name, value, url: origin });
  };
  const open = async (pathname, ready) => {
    const before = browser.state.loads;
    await browser.send('Page.navigate', { url: origin + pathname });
    const until = Date.now() + 30000;
    while (browser.state.loads === before && Date.now() < until) await sleep(100);
    await browser.waitFor(ready, pathname);
    await sleep(800);
    await browser.evaluate(`window.print=()=>{window.__printed=(window.__printed||0)+1;};window.prompt=()=>'Synthetic treatment';window.confirm=()=>true;true`);
  };
  const click = (selector) => browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const el=document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(selector)});if(!el)throw new Error('missing ${selector.replace(/'/g, '')}');el.click();return true})()`);
  const typeInto = (selector, value) => browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const el=document.querySelector(${JSON.stringify(selector)});el.value=${JSON.stringify(value)};el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));return true})()`);
  const ticked = () => browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-colour-boxes .is-ticked')?.dataset.colour||''`);
  const lmpFor = (weeks) => new Date(Date.parse(today) - weeks * 7 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  // A pregnancy through the browser: open, fill page 2, today's visit.
  const pregnancy = async (key, { age, lmpWeeks, hb, births = '1' }) => {
    await open(`/anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, `Boolean(document.querySelector('[data-action="open"], #anc-visit-form'))`);
    if (await browser.evaluate(`Boolean(document.querySelector('[data-action="open"]'))`)) {
      await click('[data-action="open"]');
      await browser.waitFor(`Boolean(document.querySelector('#anc-visit-form'))`, 'record opened');
    }
    await browser.waitFor(`document.querySelector('#anc-tasks details')?.open===true`, 'inline checklist opens');
    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-tasks-dialog')?.open`), false, 'no automatic modal');
    await click('#anc-tasks summary');
    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-tasks details').open`), false);
    await click('#anc-tasks summary');
    assert.ok(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelectorAll('#anc-tasks .anc-task').length>0`), 'to-do panel always visible');
    await typeInto('[data-path="identity.age"]', age);
    await typeInto('[data-path="identity.births"]', births);
    await typeInto('[data-path="identity.miscarriages"]', '0');
    await typeInto('[data-path="identity.abortions"]', '0');
    await typeInto('[data-path="lmp"]', lmpLater(lmpWeeks));
    await typeInto('[data-path="heightCm"]', '158');
    await typeInto('[data-path="prePregnancyWeightKg"]', '52');
    await typeInto('[data-path="labs.0.date"]', today);
    await typeInto('[data-path="labs.0.hbsag"]', 'Negative');
    await typeInto('[data-path="labs.0.group"]', 'O+');
    await typeInto('[data-visit="rapid.hiv"]', 'Negative');
    await typeInto('[data-visit="rapid.syphilis"]', 'Negative');
    await typeInto('[data-visit="rapid.hb"]', hb);
    await completeAncRequired(browser);
    await click('[data-action="save-visit"]');
    await browser.waitFor(`(document.getElementById('anc-status').textContent||'').length>1 && document.getElementById('anc-status').textContent!=='…'`, 'visit saved');
    await sleep(500);
  };
  const lmpLater = (weeks) => lmpFor(weeks);

  try {
    // ---------- Midwife queue ----------
    const green = await visit('B426-G1', 'anc');
    const yellow = await visit('B426-Y1', 'anc', { age: '40' });
    const red = await visit('B426-R1', 'anc');
    const vaccine = await visit('B426-V1', 'vaccination', { sex: 'ប្រុស', age: '1' });
    const cervix = await visit('B426-C1', 'cervix-screening', { age: '35' });
    const safe = await visit('B426-S1', 'safe-abortion');
    await useAccount('nurse_obstetric');
    await open('/maternal', `document.querySelectorAll('#queue-rows a.queue-action').length>=5`);
    const queue = await browser.evaluate(`[...document.querySelectorAll('#queue-rows a.queue-action')].map(a=>a.getAttribute('href'))`);
    assert.ok(queue.includes(`/anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(green)}`));
    assert.ok(queue.includes(`/vaccination?patient=${encodeURIComponent(vaccine)}`), 'free vaccination in the queue at once');
    assert.ok(!queue.some((href) => href.includes(encodeURIComponent(safe))), 'safe abortion is not the midwife');
    assert.ok(await browser.evaluate(`[...document.querySelectorAll('#emr-main-nav a, nav a')].some(a=>a.getAttribute('href')==='/maternal')||true`));

    // ---------- Green, yellow, red ----------
    await pregnancy(green, { age: '25', lmpWeeks: 27, hb: '12' });
    assert.equal(await ticked(), 'green', 'green pregnancy');
    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-tasks').innerText`), /មិនមែនជាការពិនិត្យតាមកាលវិភាគ|Not a scheduled visit/, 'green at 27 weeks: not a scheduled visit');
    await pregnancy(yellow, { age: '40', lmpWeeks: 26, hb: '12' });
    assert.equal(await ticked(), 'yellow', 'yellow pregnancy');
    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-tasks').innerText`), /OGTT/, 'yellow at 26 weeks: OGTT');
    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-summary').innerText`), /CPA2/);
    await pregnancy(red, { age: '25', lmpWeeks: 20, hb: '6.5' });
    assert.equal(await ticked(), 'red', 'red pregnancy');
    // Close a task without doing it: a reason is needed.
    await click('#anc-tasks li.anc-task:not(.is-done):has(a.anc-task-text) input[data-task-done]:not([disabled])');
    await browser.waitFor(`document.querySelector('#anc-close-dialog')?.open===true`, 'close dialog');
    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const d=document.querySelector('#anc-close-dialog');d.querySelector('input[value="ផ្សេងៗ"]').checked=true;d.querySelector('form').requestSubmit();return true})()`);
    await browser.waitFor(`(document.querySelector('#anc-close-dialog .anc-error')?.textContent||'').length>0`, 'other needs a text');
    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const d=document.querySelector('#anc-close-dialog');d.querySelector('input[name="anc-close-text"]').value='Synthetic reason';d.querySelector('form').requestSubmit();return true})()`);
    await browser.waitFor(`document.querySelector('#anc-close-dialog')?.open===false`, 'task closed');
    // Referral: the midwife asks; the Obstetric doctor signs and prints.
    await click('[data-action="referral"]');
    await browser.waitFor(`Boolean(document.querySelector('.anc-pending'))`, 'referral waiting for a doctor');
    await click('[data-action="print"]');
    assert.ok(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelectorAll('#anc-print .anc-print-page').length>=2`), 'printed pages 2-5');
    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-print').innerText`), /ប្រវត្តិមានផ្ទៃពោះលើកមុន/);
    const evidence = path.resolve('verification-v431');
    fs.mkdirSync(evidence, { recursive: true });
    const screenshot = async name => {
      const result = await browser.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png', captureBeyondViewport: false });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(evidence, name + '.png'), Buffer.from(result.data, 'base64'));
    };
    await screenshot('anc-desktop');
    await browser.send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 390, height: 844, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: true });
    await sleep(400);
    await screenshot('anc-mobile');
    await browser.send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 1440, height: 900, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: false });
    await browser.send('Emulation.setEmulatedMedia', { media: 'print' });
    await screenshot('anc-print');
    const pdf = await browser.send('Page.printToPDF', { printBackground: true, preferCSSPageSize: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(evidence, 'anc.pdf'), Buffer.from(pdf.data, 'base64'));
    await browser.send('Emulation.setEmulatedMedia', { media: '' });
    await useAccount('doctor_obstetric');
    await open(`/anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(red)}`, `Boolean(document.querySelector('[data-action="sign"]'))`);
    await click('[data-action="sign"]');
    await browser.waitFor(`Boolean(document.querySelector('[data-action="print-referral"]'))`, 'signed');
    await click('[data-action="print-referral"]');
    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#anc-print').innerText`), /CPA3/, 'referral letter printed');

    // ---------- Second visit of the green pregnancy (same Health ID) ----------
    const green2 = await visit('B426-G2', 'anc', { healthId: 'H-B426-G1', previous: green });
    await useAccount('nurse_obstetric');
    await open(`/anc-record?patient=${encodeURIComponent(green2)}`, `Boolean(document.querySelector('#anc-visit-form'))`);
    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelectorAll('.anc-visit-table tbody tr').length`), 1, 'the first visit is in the visit table');
    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('[data-path="lmp"]').value`), lmpFor(27), 'same pregnancy record');

    // ---------- Vaccination ----------
    await open(`/vaccination?patient=${encodeURIComponent(vaccine)}`, `Boolean(document.querySelector('#vac-form'))`);
    await browser.evaluate(`(()=>{const s=document.querySelector('#vac-form [name="vaccineId"]');s.value=[...s.options].find(o=>/BCG/.test(o.textContent)).value;s.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));document.querySelector('#vac-form').requestSubmit();return true})()`);
    await browser.waitFor(`document.querySelectorAll('#vac-history tbody tr td').length>2`, 'vaccination saved');
    assert.match(await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#vac-history').innerText`), /BCG/);

    // ---------- Cervical screening ----------
    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(cervix)}&view=opd`, `Boolean(document.querySelector('#cervix-result-panel'))`);
    // v47 removes the duplicate consultation header; check the canonical banner.
    await browser.waitFor("document.getElementById('opd_banner_patient_name')?.textContent.includes('B426-C1')", 'canonical patient identity');
    assert.equal(await browser.evaluate("Boolean(document.getElementById('emr-consultation-workspace'))"), false);
    await screenshot('patient-desktop');
    await browser.send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 390, height: 844, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: true });
    await screenshot('patient-mobile');
    await browser.send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 1440, height: 900, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: false });
    // The record opens in view mode; the midwife presses Edit (v37.8).
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 10 && await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('#cervix-send').disabled`); attempt++) {
      await browser.evaluate(`document.querySelector('[data-editor-toggle]')?.click();true`);
      await sleep(1000);
    }
    await typeInto('#cervix-result', 'positive');
    // The OPD tab still has empty required fields. Sending the screening result
    // saves nothing into the record, so it must not be blocked by them (v42.6).
    await click('#cervix-send');
    try { await browser.waitFor(`(document.querySelector('#cervix-status').textContent||'').length>3`, 'result sent'); }
    catch (error) { throw new Error(error.message + ' ' + JSON.stringify(await browser.evaluate(`({status:document.querySelector('#cervix-status').textContent,value:document.querySelector('#cervix-result').value,inert:document.querySelector('#cervix-result-panel').closest('[inert]')?.id||'',disabled:document.querySelector('#cervix-send').disabled,panels:document.querySelectorAll('#cervix-result-panel').length,pk:window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.()?.patientKey,role:window.emrSession?.user?.activeRole})`)) + JSON.stringify(browser.state.errors)); }
    assert.equal(app.database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM staff_messages WHERE destination_code='gynecology' AND patient_key=?").get(cervix).n, 1);

    // ---------- Safe abortion: Gynecology ward ----------
    await useAccount('doctor_gynecology');
    await open('/maternal?kind=gynecology', `document.querySelectorAll('#queue-rows a.queue-action').length>=1`);
    assert.ok((await browser.evaluate(`[...document.querySelectorAll('#queue-rows a.queue-action')].map(a=>a.getAttribute('href'))`)).includes(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(safe)}&view=opd`));
    await open(`/emr?patient=${encodeURIComponent(safe)}&view=opd`, `Boolean(document.getElementById('sub_opd'))`);

    assert.deepEqual(browser.state.errors, [], 'no JavaScript errors');
  } finally {
    browser.close();
    app.server.closeAllConnections();
    await new Promise(resolve => app.server.close(resolve));
    app.database.close();
  }
});

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/v475-anc-task-auto-done.test.mjs =====
// v47.5 - The ANC to-do list is ticked from the data entered by Registration,
// Triage, the midwife, the Laboratory and Imaging. Synthetic records only.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import * as anc from '../public/anc-engine.mjs';

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const read = (file) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, file), 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
const chart = JSON.parse(read('server/catalogues/anc-color-chart.json'));
const schedule = JSON.parse(read('server/catalogues/anc-visit-schedule.json'));
const TODAY = '2026-10-07';
const negative = [{ date: '2026-09-01', hiv: 'Negative', syphilis: 'Negative', hbsag: 'Negative', hb: '12', group: 'O+' }];
const base = (weeks, extra = {}) => ({ identity: { age: '25', births: '1', miscarriages: '0', abortions: '0' }, lmp: anc.addDays(TODAY, -weeks * 7),
  heightCm: '158', prePregnancyWeightKg: '52', labs: negative, history: {}, ...extra });
const vitals = { bp: '110/70', pulse: '80', temperature: '36.8', spo2: '98' };
const status = (record, visit = {}, more = {}) => anc.ancStatus({ chart, schedule, record, visits: [...(more.earlier || []), { date: TODAY, ...vitals, ...visit }], today: TODAY, vaccinations: more.vaccinations || [] });
const task = (result, action) => result.tasks.find((item) => item.action === action);
const done = (result, action) => { const found = task(result, action); assert.ok(found, `task ${action} is on this visit`); return found.autoDone; };

test('first visit: record tasks always done; weight, height, BMI from Triage; vital signs need BP, pulse, temperature and SpO2', () => {
  const first = status(base(10), { weight: '52' });
  assert.equal(first.visit.visit, 1);
  assert.equal(first.tasks.filter((item) => item.action === 'auto:record').every((item) => item.autoDone), true);
  assert.equal(first.tasks.filter((item) => item.action === 'auto:record').length, 2, 'ANC record and digital system; the paper books stay manual');
  assert.equal(done(first, 'data:weight'), true);
  assert.equal(done(first, 'data:height'), true);
  assert.equal(done(first, 'data:bmi'), true);
  assert.equal(done(first, 'data:vitals'), true);
  assert.equal(done(status(base(10), { spo2: '' }), 'data:vitals'), false, 'SpO2 missing');
  assert.equal(done(status(base(10), { weight: '' }), 'data:weight'), false);
  assert.equal(done(status(base(10, { prePregnancyWeightKg: '' })), 'data:bmi'), false);
});

test('page 2 answers tick anaemia (visit 1), breast and mouth examinations', () => {
  const unanswered = status(base(10));
  for (const action of ['data:anaemia', 'data:breast', 'data:mouth']) assert.equal(done(unanswered, action), false, action);
  const history = { anaemia: { eyelid: 'no', nails: 'no', palms: 'yes' }, mouth: { stomatitis: 'no', caries: 'yes', breast: 'no' } };
  const answered = status(base(10, { history }));
  for (const action of ['data:anaemia', 'data:breast', 'data:mouth']) assert.equal(done(answered, action), true, action);
  assert.equal(done(status(base(10, { history: { anaemia: { eyelid: 'no' } } })), 'data:anaemia'), false, 'all three questions needed');
});

test('today\'s exam: FHR, fundal height, presentation, urine, iron, education; anaemia from today\'s Hb after visit 1', () => {
  const later = (visit) => status(base(24), visit, { earlier: [{ date: anc.addDays(TODAY, -14 * 7), weight: '50' }] });
  const empty = later({});
  assert.equal(empty.visit.visit, 2);
  for (const action of ['data:fhr', 'data:uh', 'data:fp', 'data:education', 'rx:iron', 'data:anaemia']) assert.equal(done(empty, action), false, action);
  const full = later({ fhr: '140', uh: '24', fp: 'cephalic', fer: '30', educationDone: true, rapid: { hb: '11' }, proteinuria: '-' });
  for (const action of ['data:fhr', 'data:uh', 'data:fp', 'data:education', 'rx:iron', 'data:anaemia', 'own:urine']) assert.equal(done(full, action), true, action);
  assert.equal(done(later({ fer: '0' }), 'rx:iron'), false, '0 tablets: not given');
});

test('tetanus: a dose recorded today or the tetanus field "complete"', () => {
  const later = (visit, vaccinations) => status(base(24), visit, { vaccinations });
  assert.equal(done(later({}, []), 'vaccine:tt'), false);
  assert.equal(done(later({}, [{ givenDate: TODAY, doseNumber: 2 }]), 'vaccine:tt'), true);
  assert.equal(done(later({}, [{ givenDate: '2025-01-01', doseNumber: 1 }]), 'vaccine:tt'), false, 'an old dose is not this visit');
  assert.equal(done(later({ legacyDetails: { 44: '\u1782\u17d2\u179a\u1794\u17cb' } }, []), 'vaccine:tt'), true);
});

test('second CBC is not ticked by the first Hb; an Hb within that visit\'s weeks ticks it', () => {
  const plan = schedule.yellowRed;
  const cbc2 = plan.visits.find((visit) => [...(visit.lab || [])].some((text) => /\u179b\u17be\u1780\u1791\u17b8\u17e2/.test(text)));
  assert.ok(cbc2, 'the schedule has CBC 2');
  const weeks = cbc2.gaFrom;
  const yellow = (labs, visit = {}) => status(base(weeks, { identity: { age: '40' }, labs }), visit);
  const old = yellow(negative);
  assert.equal(old.visit.visit, cbc2.visit);
  assert.equal(done(old, 'own:hb-window'), false, 'Hb of 2026-09-01 is before this visit');
  assert.equal(done(yellow([...negative, { date: TODAY, hb: '11.5' }]), 'own:hb-window'), true);
  assert.equal(done(yellow(negative, { rapid: { hb: '11' } }), 'own:hb-window'), true, 'rapid Hb today');
});

test('OGTT: a released glucose result within the visit\'s weeks', () => {
  const visit = schedule.yellowRed.visits.find((item) => (item.lab || []).some((text) => /OGTT/.test(text)));
  const yellow = (labs) => status(base(visit.gaFrom, { identity: { age: '40' }, labs }));
  assert.equal(done(yellow(negative), 'order:OGTT'), false);
  assert.equal(done(yellow([...negative, { date: TODAY, glucose: '5.1' }]), 'order:OGTT'), true);
  assert.match(read('server/maternal.mjs'), /OGTT: 'glucose', GTT: 'glucose', GLU: 'glucose'/);
});

test('ultrasound: dating scan at visit 1; later scans within the visit\'s weeks', () => {
  assert.equal(done(status(base(12)), 'order:ULTRASOUND'), false);
  assert.equal(done(status(base(12, { ultrasounds: [{ date: TODAY, ga: '12' }] })), 'order:ULTRASOUND'), true);
  assert.equal(done(status(base(12, { ultrasoundEdd: '2027-04-21', ultrasoundDate: TODAY })), 'order:ULTRASOUND'), true);
  const later = (ultrasounds) => status(base(24, { ultrasounds }));
  assert.equal(done(later([{ date: anc.addDays(TODAY, -12 * 7), ga: '12' }]), 'order:ULTRASOUND'), false, 'the dating scan is not the growth scan');
  assert.equal(done(later([{ date: anc.addDays(TODAY, -3), ga: '24' }]), 'order:ULTRASOUND'), true);
});

test('overdue: an earlier visit saved in its weeks is not overdue; manual and education tasks still are', () => {
  const lmp = anc.addDays(TODAY, -24 * 7);
  const firstDate = anc.addDays(lmp, 10 * 7);
  const record = base(24, { history: { anaemia: { eyelid: 'no', nails: 'no', palms: 'no' }, mouth: { stomatitis: 'no', caries: 'no', breast: 'no' } } });
  const missed = status(record, {}, {});
  const seen = status(record, {}, { earlier: [{ date: firstDate, weight: '50', ...vitals, proteinuria: '-' }] });
  const overdueActions = (result) => result.overdue.map((item) => item.action);
  assert.ok(overdueActions(missed).includes('data:weight'));
  assert.equal(overdueActions(seen).includes('data:weight'), false);
  assert.equal(overdueActions(seen).includes('data:vitals'), false);
  assert.ok(overdueActions(seen).includes('data:education'), 'no education ticked at visit 1');
});

test('page and server: education box, tick box saving, vaccines and the CBC 2 focus', () => {
  const page = read('public/anc-record.js');
  assert.match(page, /data-visit="educationDone"/);
  assert.match(page, /anc\.educationDone/);
  assert.match(page, /setPath\(draft, target\.dataset\.visit, target\.type === 'checkbox' \? target\.checked : target\.value\)/);
  assert.match(page, /vaccinations: data\.vaccinations \|\| \[\]/);
  assert.match(page, /'own:hb-window': \(\) => '\[data-visit="rapid\.hb"\]'/);
  const server = read('server/maternal.mjs');
  assert.match(server, /'legacyDetails', 'educationDone'\]/);
  assert.match(server, /vaccinations: pregnancyVaccinations\(database, pregnancy\)/);
});
===== END FILE: tests/v475-anc-task-auto-done.test.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/v476-anc-clean-layout.test.mjs =====
// v47.6 - Clean ANC screen layout: fixed grid of cells, BP and pulse apart,
// tick boxes lined up, fixed table columns; screen only (print keeps its flow).
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const read = (file) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, file), 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
const page = read('public/anc-record.js');
const css = read('public/anc-record.css');
const v476 = css.slice(css.indexOf('/* v47.6'));

test('today\'s visit: every field is a cell; blood pressure and pulse are two fields with units', () => {
  assert.match(page, /const cell = \(labelHtml, control, \{ unit = '', span = 1, check = false, after = '' \} = \{\}\) =>/);
  assert.match(page, /cell\(esc\(t\('anc\.bp', 'Blood pressure'\)\), vfield\('bp', \{ size: 'small' \}\), \{ unit: 'mmHg' \}\)/);
  assert.match(page, /cell\(esc\(t\('anc\.pulse', 'Pulse'\)\), vfield\('pulse', \{ size: 'tiny' \}\), \{ unit: perMinute\(\) \}\)/);
  assert.equal(/\$\{vfield\('bp', \{ size: 'small' \}\)\} - \$\{vfield\('pulse'/.test(page), false, 'pulse no longer follows BP on one line');
  assert.match(page, /cellLabel\.append\(marker\)/, 'required star stays in the label');
  assert.match(read('public/new-text-data.js'), /"anc\.bp": "\u179f\u1798\u17d2\u1796\u17b6\u1792\u1788\u17b6\u1798"/);
  assert.match(read('public/new-text-data.js'), /"anc\.pulse": "\u1787\u17b8\u1796\u1785\u179a"/);
});

test('page 2: identity cells, lined-up tick boxes, yes / no and numbered rows', () => {
  assert.match(page, /cell\(esc\(id\.eddUltrasound\), field\('ultrasoundEdd'/);
  assert.equal((page.match(/class="anc-check-grid"/g) || []).length, 3, 'general, family and social history');
  assert.match(page, /anc-yesno-row"><span class="anc-yesno-q">/);
  assert.match(page, /anc-entry anc-entry-surgery/);
  assert.match(page, /anc-entry anc-entry-allergy/);
  assert.match(page, /anc-fixed-table anc-prev-table/);
  assert.match(page, /anc-fixed-table anc-us-table/);
});

test('styles: 4 / 2 / 1 columns, aligned cells and tick boxes, screen only', () => {
  assert.ok(v476.startsWith('/* v47.6'));
  assert.match(v476, /^@media screen \{/m);
  assert.equal(/@media print/.test(v476), false, 'the print layout is not changed');
  assert.match(v476, /\.anc-page \.anc-grid, \.anc-page \.anc-visit-grid\.anc-grid, \.anc-page \.anc-identity \{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat\(4,minmax\(0,1fr\)\)/);
  assert.match(v476, /\.anc-page \.anc-cell \{ display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch;/);
  assert.match(v476, /max-width:1100px\) \{\n \.anc-page \.anc-grid[^}]*repeat\(2,minmax\(0,1fr\)\)/);
  assert.match(v476, /max-width:760px\) \{\n \.anc-page \.anc-grid[^}]*grid-template-columns:minmax\(0,1fr\)/);
  assert.match(v476, /\.anc-page \.anc-choice \{ display:flex; align-items:flex-start;/);
  assert.match(v476, /\.anc-page \.anc-fixed-table \{ table-layout:fixed; \}/);
});
===== END FILE: tests/v476-anc-clean-layout.test.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: tests/v477-anc-clean-checklist.test.mjs =====
// v47.7 - The ANC to-do list is a clean checklist (tick box + text, grouped,
// no buttons). The text opens the field to fill; orders sit next to the fields.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const read = (file) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, file), 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
const page = read('public/anc-record.js');
const css = read('public/anc-record.css');
const taskItem = page.slice(page.indexOf('function taskItem('), page.indexOf('function taskGroups('));

test('a task is a tick box and its text; no buttons in the list', () => {
  assert.equal(/function taskButton/.test(page), false);
  assert.equal(/<button/.test(taskItem), false, 'no button in a task row');
  assert.match(taskItem, /<a href="#" class="anc-task-text" data-task-go=/);
  assert.match(taskItem, /data-auto="1" aria-readonly="true"/, 'data ticks stay green (locked, not disabled)');
  assert.equal(/data-task-close|data-task-reopen|data-focus/.test(page), false, 'old list buttons are gone');
  for (const key of ['records', 'lab', 'exam', 'ultrasound', 'care']) assert.match(page, new RegExp(`\\['${key}', 'anc\\.cat\\.${key}'`));
});

test('the text opens the field where the task is filled', () => {
  for (const [action, target] of [['data:weight', '[data-visit="weight"]'], ['data:vitals', '[data-visit="bp"]'], ['own:hiv', '[data-visit="rapid.hiv"]'],
    ['data:breast', '[data-path="history.mouth.breast"]'], ['data:height', '[data-path="heightCm"]'], ['vaccine:tt', '[data-visit="legacyDetails.44"]']]) {
    assert.ok(page.includes(`'${action}': () => '${target}'`), action);
  }
  assert.match(page, /'order:ULTRASOUND': \(\) => firstEmpty\('\[data-path\^="ultrasounds\."\]\[data-path\$="\.date"\]'\)/);
  assert.match(page, /if \(button\.dataset\.taskGo\) \{ event\.preventDefault\(\); const task = shownTasks\.get/);
  assert.match(page, /place\.classList\.add\('anc-flash'\)/);
});

test('ticking: a data task asks the reason; education ticks today\'s box; cancel shows the saved state', () => {
  assert.match(page, /if \(hasData\(task\)\) \{ event\.target\.checked = false; askCloseReason\(key\); return; \}/);
  assert.match(page, /task\.action === 'data:education'\) \{\n\s+draft\.educationDone = true;/);
  assert.match(page, /dialog\.addEventListener\('close', \(\) => refreshLive\(\), \{ once: true \}\);/);
});

test('orders are next to their fields and are not printed', () => {
  assert.match(page, /const LAB_ORDERS = \{ hbsag: 'HBSAG', hb: 'CBC', group: 'BLOOD_GROUP' \};/);
  assert.match(page, /OGTT \$\{orderButton\('OGTT'\)\}/);
  assert.match(page, /\$\{esc\(u\.title\)\} \$\{orderButton\('ULTRASOUND'\)\}/);
  assert.match(page, /data-action="rx-iron"/);
  assert.match(page, /vaccine=tt">\$\{esc\(t\('anc\.giveVaccine'/);
  assert.match(page, /\.anc-rapid, \.anc-order, \.anc-order-line'\)\.forEach\(\(node\) => node\.remove\(\)\)/);
});

test('styles: 3 / 2 / 1 columns, plain text (screen only)', () => {
  const v477 = css.slice(css.indexOf('/* v47.7'));
  assert.match(v477, /^@media screen \{/m);
  assert.equal(/@media print/.test(v477), false);
  assert.match(v477, /\.anc-tasks \.anc-task-list \{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat\(3,minmax\(0,1fr\)\)/);
  assert.match(v477, /\.anc-tasks \.anc-task-text \{ all:unset;/);
  assert.match(v477, /max-width:1100px\) \{ \.anc-tasks \.anc-task-list \{ grid-template-columns:repeat\(2,minmax\(0,1fr\)\)/);
});
===== END FILE: tests/v477-anc-clean-checklist.test.mjs =====
